[APT:COMLINKHELPTEXTFRIENDS]

[APT:CONNECTIONINSTRUCTIONS]
Use this screen to evaluate peer-to-peer connections between players.  A player that is unable to connect to other players will either have 'Failed Connection' or will be taking a long time in 'Attempting Connection'.  If the connection does not fix, please use the Messenger to advise the player to adjust firewall or port settings. If the problem cannot be addressed, you may want to kick the player out of the session.  'Waiting to Connect' is not bad, it just means that another connection has not been resolved.  Players with a poor ping indicator can also adversely affect game play.
使用这个画面评估玩家之间的点对点连接。无法与其他玩家连接的玩家，会看到“无法连接”或是看到“正在尝试连接”字样很长时间。如果连接问题未能解决，请设法告知玩家调整防火墙或通讯端口设置。如果问题无法解决，您可能得将该玩家踢出游戏局。“等候连接”并非发生问题，而是表示其他的连接还在确定中。Ping 值不佳的玩家也会对游戏顺畅度造成负面影响。

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_1]
An overview of the most rudimentary basics of surveying battle and ordering forces. Featuring such classics as camera panning and the attack-move command!
描述勘察战场和指挥部队的初步要领。例如移动视角和行进攻击命令等等！

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_2]
An overview of how to construct a military base as the Soviets, Allies, or Empire of the Rising Sun. Thrill to their unique building mechanics!
描述如何建造苏联、盟军或旭日帝国军事基地。感受其独特的建造机制！

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_3]
An overview of various military units and their unique special abilities. Your gateway to learning the strengths and vulnerabilities of all combat forces!
描述各类军事单位及其独特特殊能力。这是了解所有战斗单位优缺点的入门之选！

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_4]
An overview of Top-Secret Protocols, special commander abilities authorized for use in battle. Master these abilities to keep the odds in your favor!
描述最高机密协议——战斗中授权使用的特殊指挥官技能.掌握这些技能，让胜利向您倾斜！

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_5]
An overview of advanced combat tactics, including alternate stances, move commands, and much more. Once you've grasped the basics, here's your next stop!
高级战斗战术的概述，包括可切换的形态、移动指令等。掌握了基础之后，这就是您的下一站！

[APT:LESSONSUMMARY_6]
An overview of how to collaborate with a co-commander, who will be supporting you in the campaign. Learn about command directives and special tactics!
描述如何与战役中支援您的合作指挥官协同作战。学习指挥指令和特殊战术！

Somewhere in the arteries of a vast Imperial research facility, an unusual young woman awakens from a long and terrible slumber fraught with nightmares she knows were real...

More bloodshed awaited Yuriko after her failed escape attempt. In her defiant act, the Imperial Shogunate found evidence of the Omega Program's successes. So they gave her a new surname, a psychic dampener that controlled her temper, and a job defending her nation's front lines. And there she failed again.
百合子逃脱失败后，等待她的是更多的腥风血雨。她那次反抗之举，却让帝国幕府找到了欧米伽计划成功的证据。于是，他们给了她一个新姓氏、一个能压制情绪的心灵抑制器，以及一份保卫国家前线的工作——而在那里，她又一次失败了。

[APT:Y03_OVERVIEW]
Yuriko never wanted to see this place again, though she had no choice but to return. She reassures herself that this will be the last time. And then she prepares to smash through the roof. This was never her home, nor would it be her sister's.
百合子再也不想见到这个地方，尽管她别无选择，只能回来。她安慰自己，这是最后一次了。随即，她准备破顶而出。这里从来就不是她的家，以后也不会是她姐妹的家。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_ALLIEDFIRSTASSAULT]
A no-nonsense commander who's never cracked under pressure, even when the Soviets captured him as a prisoner of war. He fought his way out bare-fisted, so imagine what he can do with tanks on his side.
一位不苟言笑的指挥官，即使沦为苏军战俘也从未在压力下屈服。他曾赤手空拳打出一条生路，可想而知，当有坦克助阵时他又会有多强。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_ALLIEDSPECIALFORCES]
A former spy who showed exceptional talent for high-level command. Despite her cheerful demeanor, she has deep insight into enemy tactics and priority access to the Allies' latest special weapons.
曾是一名间谍，却展现出卓越的指挥才能。尽管外表开朗，她对敌人的战术有着深刻的理解，并能优先使用盟军最新的特种武器。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_ALLIEDSQUADRONLEADER]
A sharp-witted hotshot pilot who traded in his wings for a shot at a leadership position. Now with multiple squadrons at his disposal, he's racking up successful sorties at a rapid rate.
一位机智的王牌飞行员，放弃了飞行生涯，换取担任领导职务的机会。如今他掌管着多支中队，出击记录正快速攀升。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_JAPANAMBUSHDIVISION]
A highly experienced and even-tempered commander, and the latest in the long line of Imperial military leaders who have worked for generations to ensure Japan's domination over the entire world.
一位经验丰富、性情沉稳的指挥官，出身于世代为帝国效力的军事家族，其使命就是确保帝国称霸世界。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_JAPANFLEETCOMMAND]
A stern commander who has spent much of her life on the high seas, mastering the art of naval warfare. Many of the Empire of the Rising Sun's finest warships have sworn to serve her to the death.
一位严厉的指挥官，大半生驰骋于大海之上，精通海战艺术。许多旭日帝国的精锐战舰都发誓愿为她效死。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_JAPANMECHAWARFARE]
Kenji's exceptional understanding of the Empire's advanced military technology as well as his precise and dexterous command over military forces are just two of the reasons why he feels justified in his vanity.
贤治对帝国先进军事科技的非凡理解，以及他对部队精准而娴熟的指挥才能，只是他引以为傲的众多理由之一。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_SOVIETAIRMARSHALL]
Having achieved ace status at a young age, Zhana now leads many of the Union's finest fighter squadrons. She is a proud patriot from a famous military family loyal to the Union's ideals.
夏娜在年轻时便已取得王牌飞行员称号，如今她指挥着联盟多支精锐战斗机中队。她是一位自豪的爱国者，出身名门军事世家，忠于联盟的理想。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_SOVIETHEAVYARMOR]
A former tank commander himself, Oleg now leads some of the Union's mightiest armor divisions. His men tend to appreciate his lack of formality, though his superiors sometimes don't.
欧列格曾是坦克指挥官出身，如今领导着苏联最强大的装甲师。他的部下往往欣赏他不拘礼节，不过他的上级有时就不太喜欢了。

[COMMANDERDESCRIPTION:COOP_SOVIETSHOCKSPECIALIST]
An intense, up-and-coming young Soviet commander responsible for an unusually high number of confirmed enemy kills. He is always eager to field-test the Union's experimental forces.
一名充满干劲、崭露头角的年轻苏联指挥官，战场杀敌数高得惊人。他总热衷于将苏联的实验性部队投入实战测试。

[DESC:ACTIVATEMAGNETICARMOR]
Supercharges defensive strength while drawing incoming fire. Cannot attack in the meantime.
在吸引来袭火力的同时大幅提升防御强度。持续期间无法攻击。

[DESC:ACTIVATEMAGNETICARMOROFF]
Stops automatically absorbing all incoming fire in order to keep using the main gun.
停止自动吸收所有来袭炮火，以便继续使用主炮。

[DESC:ACTIVATEMIRAGEFIELD]
Projects a camo field that cloaks friendly units. Cannot self-disguise or fire in this mode.
生成能让友军单位隐形的迷彩力场。此模式下无法伪装自身或开火。

[DESC:ACTIVATEMIRAGEFIELDOFF]
Reverts to combat-and-ambush config. Gains unassuming camouflage when idle.
恢复为战斗伏击配置。静止时进入低辨识度伪装状态。

[DESC:ACTIVATESHORTCIRCUIT]
Radiates a disruption field that stops nearby enemy vehicles... and the Tesla Trooper.
释放干扰场，能使附近敌方车辆停止运转……就连磁爆步兵自身也会受到影响。

[DESC:ACTIVATESHORTCIRCUITOFF]

[DESC:ACTIVATESUPERSONICABILITY]

[DESC:ACTIVATETARGETPAINTER]
Switches to using a targeting laser that makes foes much more vulnerable to attack.
切换为瞄准激光，使目标更容易受到攻击。

[DESC:ACTIVATETARGETPAINTEROFF]
Switches to using the 90 mm smoothbore gun, which is effective against enemy armor.
切换为90毫米滑膛炮，可有效对付敌方装甲目标。

[DESC:AGGRESIVESTANCE]
Units in Aggressive Stance will automatically fire on enemy structures as well as units, and will indefinitely pursue enemies.
处于侵略姿态下的部队会自动攻击敌方建筑和单位，并会持续追击敌人。

[DESC:AIASSAULTDIRECTIVE]
Select a location: Your ally will build up forces to eliminate foes there and along the way.

[DESC:AIHOLDOBJECTDIRECTIVE]
Select an enemy: Your ally will immediately send forces to deal with it and other hostiles there.

[DESC:AIHOLDPOSITIONDIRECTIVE]
Select a location: Your ally will immediately send forces to regroup there and hold it down.
选择一个位置：您的盟友将立即派兵到该处重新集结并固守。

[DESC:AINORMALDIRECTIVE]
Your ally will command autonomously: This cancels any other orders you've given.
您的盟友将自主行动，此命令将取消您已下达的任何其他指令。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_AIRCRAFTCARRIER]
Aircraft Carriers are powerful naval artillery platforms, which can neutralize enemy installations from a safe distance.[6.6]Their combat drones automatically return to rearm between bombing runs.[10.9]Carriers can also launch the Blackout Missile, which deactivates vehicles or structures caught in its blast.
航空母舰是强大的海上炮击平台，能在安全距离外压制敌方设施。[6.6]其作战无人机在轰炸任务完成后会自动返航补充弹药。[10.9]航母还可发射断电导弹，使爆炸范围内的车辆或建筑瘫痪。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_APOLLOFIGHTER]
Apollo Fighters are air-superiority jets, armed with rapid-fire cannons that can tear enemy aircraft to bits.[6.1]Their special ability is to quickly return to their Airbase to reload and repair as necessary.
阿波罗战斗机是空优喷气战机，装备速射机炮，能撕碎敌方飞机。[6.1]其特殊能力是必要时快速返回空军基地进行装弹和维修。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_ASSAULTDESTROYER]
Assault Destroyers are sturdy amphibious warships armed with heavy cannons effective against armored targets.[6.2]Instead of using their main guns, they can activate their Black-Hole Armor, which automatically draws incoming fire directed at nearby friendly forces.
突袭驱逐舰是坚固的两栖战舰，装备对装甲目标有效的重型火炮。[6.2]除了主炮之外，它们还可以启动黑洞装甲，自动将附近友军受到的攻击吸引到自己身上。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_ATHENACANNON]
Athena Cannons are mobile artillery units linked to our space defense network.[4.2]They can pinpoint targets from long range, causing a proton beam to fire down from orbit.[9.1]Their special ability is the Aegis shield, a barrier that temporarily protects the Athena cannon and nearby units.[15.3]Athena Cannons cannot act while the shield is up.
雅典娜炮是与我方太空防御网络连接的机动火炮单位。[4.2]它们可从远距离精准锁定目标，引导轨道上的质子光束轰击地面。[9.1]其特殊技能是能量护盾，一道能暂时保护雅典娜炮及附近单位的屏障。[15.3]护盾开启期间，雅典娜炮无法行动。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_ATTACKDOG]
Attack Dogs are trained for amphibious scouting and light combat.[3.1]Their attack is lethal to most infantry.[5]They can detect disguised enemy units as well.[7.8]Their special ability is a deafening bark that paralyzes enemy infantry within earshot.
警犬受过两栖侦察和轻型战斗训练。[3.1]它们的攻击对大多数步兵是致命的。[5]它们也能识破伪装的敌方单位。[7.8]其特殊技能是震耳欲聋的吠叫，能使听力范围内的敌方步兵动弹不得。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_CENTURYBOMBER]
Century Bombers are well-armored flying infantry transports that can carpet-bomb enemy bases to dust.[5.5]They must rearm back at an Airbase between bombing runs, at which time they can pick up more infantry to paradrop behind enemy lines.
世纪轰炸机是装甲良好的飞行步兵运输机，能将敌方基地地毯式轰炸为灰烬。[5.5]每次轰炸任务之间必须返回空军基地进行补给。它们还可以搭载更多步兵，空降至敌后。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_CRYCOPTER]
Cryocopters are special support units which can freeze targets.[3.3]Frozen units cannot act and are highly vulnerable to attack.[7.2]The Cryocopter's special ability is the Shrink Beam, which temporarily reduces a target in size, making it less powerful and easily crushed.
冰冻直升机是能冻结目标的特殊支援单位。[3.3]被冻结的单位无法行动，不堪一击。[7.2]冰冻直升机的特殊技能是缩小光束，能暂时缩小目标，使其威力减弱且容易被碾压。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_CRYOLEGIONNAIRE]
Cryo Legionnaires are heavy assault infantry whose ice cannons can freeze multiple assailants dead in their tracks in just a few seconds.[7]The Cryo Legionnaires' special ability is the boost kick, which they use to shatter frozen targets, or leap into or out of battle.
冰冻军团是重型突击步兵，其冰冻炮可在数秒内将多名攻击者当场冻结。[7]冰冻军团的特殊能力是跳跃撞击，可以用来踢碎冻结的目标，或是跳入或跳出战斗区域。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_DOLPHIN]
The Allied navy employs specially-trained Dolphins armed with sonic weapons for scouting the seas and intercepting enemy ships.[6.9]Dolphins have the special ability to jump up and out of the water to avoid dangerous situations.
盟军海军雇用了经过特殊训练的海豚，装备声波武器巡逻海域并拦截敌军船只。[6.1]海豚的特殊技能是跃出水面，以避开危险情况。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_ENGINEER]
Though defenseless against enemy units, Engineers can capture neutral or enemy structures on land or at sea.[6.1]They can also repair your damaged structures.[8.8]Their special ability is to set up a first-aid tent, which causes any of your infantry units in the vicinity to gradually recover from injuries.
虽然工程师面对敌方单位时毫无防御能力，但他们可以占领陆地或海上的中立或敌方建筑。[6.1]他们也能修复您受损的建筑。[8.8]其特殊技能是搭建急救帐篷，使附近您的步兵单位逐渐从伤势中恢复。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_FUTURETANK]
FutureTank X-1s are heavily armored, computer-controlled combat vehicles that are virtually unmatched on the ground.[6.2]Their experimental cannons can easily cut through even the toughest armor, while their Riot Beam special weapon lets them turn the tables on enemies attacking in mass.
未来坦克X-1是装甲厚重、由计算机控制的战斗载具，在地面上几乎所向无敌。[6.2]其实验性火炮可轻易击穿最坚固的装甲，而特殊武器“暴乱光束”则能在大群敌人来袭时扭转战局。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_GUARDIANTANK]
Guardian Tanks are armed with main guns that work well against most vehicles and structures.[4.4]These tanks can even crush enemy infantry.[7.3]Their special ability is to switch to a targeting laser that reduces the armor of an enemy, making it more vulnerable to attack.
守护者坦克装备的主炮能有效对付大多数车辆和建筑。[4.4]这些坦克甚至能碾压敌方步兵。[7.3]其特殊技能是切换为瞄准激光，从而削弱敌军的装甲，，使其更容易受到攻击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_HARBINGER]
Harbinger Gunships are massive support aircraft armed with the latest in weapons technology.[5.1]Next to nothing can resist their on-board collider cannons, but just in case, they can switch to fast-firing antipersonnel guns for taking care of any smaller threats.
先锋武装战艇机是搭载最新武器科技的重型支援飞机。[5.1]其机载对撞炮几乎无人能挡，但为防万一，它们还可切换为速射反人员机枪，以应对任何小型威胁。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_HYDROFOIL]
Hydrofoils are swift vessels that can quickly cut through enemy aircraft using their rapid-firing autocannons.[5.9]Their special ability is to switch to using a Weapon Jammer, which prevents targeted foes from attacking.
水翼船是迅捷的快艇，能利用速射机炮快速击落敌方飞机。[5.9]其特殊技能是切换为武器干扰器，阻止被锁定的敌人发起攻击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_JAVELINSOLDIER]
Javelin Soldiers carry portable missile launchers effective against enemy armor and aircraft.[5.3]Their special ability is to switch to laser-guided mode, in which it takes longer to acquire a target, but grants a much faster firing rate.
标枪兵携带可有效对付敌方装甲和飞机的便携式导弹发射器。[5.3]其特殊技能是切换为激光制导模式，该模式下锁定目标所需时间更长，但能换来更快的射击速率。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_MCV]
Mobile Construction Vehicles are amphibious mobile HQs.[3.7]Though unarmed, they are the focal point of Allied military operations.[7.6]They can unpack into Construction Yards, which build all Allied structures.[11.4]Do whatever it takes to protect these facilities.
移动基地车是两栖机动指挥部。[3.7]这些车辆没有武装，但它们是盟军军事行动的核心。[7.6]它们可以展开为建造基地，用于建造所有盟军建筑。[11.4]必须不惜一切代价保护这些设施。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_MIRAGETANK]
Mirage Tanks automatically camouflage themselves as innocuous objects while immobile.[4.4]They can devastate surface targets with their spectrum cannons.[7.7]Their special ability is to switch on a device that makes nearby forces invisible to the enemy.[12.8]Mirage Tanks cannot camouflage or fire in this mode.
幻影坦克在静止时会自动伪装成无害的物体。[4.4]它们能利用光谱炮重创地面目标。[7.7]其特殊技能是切换使用使附近的友军对敌人隐形的一种装置。[12.8]此模式下幻影坦克无法伪装或开火。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_MULTIGUNNERIFV]
Multigunner IFVs are quick and versatile jack-of-all-trades vehicles that automatically switch weapons depending on which infantry unit is riding inside.[9.4]Their stock weapon is an anti-air/anti-surface rocket launcher, though they can be specialized to repair vehicles, counter enemy infantry, and more.
多功能步兵战车是快速而灵活的全能战车，能根据搭载的步兵自动切换武器。[9.4]其标配武器是防空/对地火箭发射器，但也可通过搭载不同步兵实现车辆维修、反制敌方步兵等特殊功能。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_PACIFIER]
Pacifier Field Artillery Vehicles can neutralize enemy combatants from long range using their Grand Cannons.[5.9]They must deploy to a fixed position on land to initiate bombardment.[9.8]When not deployed, Pacifiers may cross land or sea and defend themselves with heavy machineguns.
平定者战地火炮载具可利用巨型加农炮在远距离消灭敌方战斗人员。[5.9]它们必须在陆地上展开至固定位置才能进行炮击。[9.8]未展开时，平定者可跨越陆地与海洋，并以重机枪自卫。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_PEACEKEEPER]
Peacekeepers are shotgun-wielding frontline infantry who can knock enemy infantry to the ground whilst in close quarters.[7]Their special ability is to switch to using a riot shield, which provides added defense whilst allowing them to close the distance with their foes.
维和步兵是手持霰弹枪的前线步兵，能在近距离内将敌方步兵击倒在地。[7]其特殊技能是切换为防暴盾牌，在提供额外防御的同时，还能让他们拉近与敌人的距离。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_PROSPECTOR]
Prospectors are amphibious resource-collecting vehicles.[3]They are unarmed and need to be protected.[5.7]Their special ability is to unpack into Command Hubs.[8.7]Other structures may then be built nearby.[11.1]This conversion is irreversible yet essential for expanding to new locations.
采矿车是两栖资源采集车辆。[3]它们毫无武装，需要保护。[5.7]其特殊技能是展开为指挥中心。随后便可在附近建造其他建筑。[11.1]这种转变是不可逆的，但对拓展至新区域至关重要。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_RIPTIDE]
Riptide ACVs are all-terrain assault transports, with room for several infantry as well as a mounted machine gun for protection.[7.3]Riptides are more powerful at sea, where they can attack using torpedos as well as their gun.[12.1]They are useful for rushing infantry behind enemy lines.
激流ACV是全地形突击运输艇，可搭载数名步兵，并配备一挺机枪用于自卫。[7.3]激流在水上更具威力，能同时使用鱼雷和机枪攻击。[12.1]它们适合将步兵快速运送至敌后。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_SPY]
While Spies are unarmed, they can disguise themselves as other infantry and attempt to sneak into enemy bases on land or sea.[6.8]Enemy scouts can break their disguise.[9.2]They can infiltrate structures, causing the enemy base to suffer various consequences.[14]Spies can also bribe enemy units near them to join your cause, for a fee.
间谍虽然毫无武装，但能伪装成其他步兵，尝试潜入陆地或海上的敌方基地。[6.8]敌方侦察单位能识破他们的伪装。[9.2]他们能渗透建筑，使敌方基地遭受各种负面效果。[14]间谍还能贿赂附近的敌方单位，使其加入您的阵营，当然这需要花费资金。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_TANYA]
Special Agent Tanya Adams is our finest commando.[2.7]She can easily dispatch enemy infantry with her pistols, and eliminate structures and vehicles with her explosives.[8.6]Tanya is a remarkable swimmer too.[10.6]Her special ability is the Time Belt, which instantly transports her back in time several moments and can get her out of harm's way.
谭雅·亚当斯特工是我们最优秀的突击队员。[2.7]她能用手枪轻松解决敌方步兵，用炸药摧毁建筑和车辆。[8.6]谭雅还是一名出色的游泳健将。[10.6]她的特殊技能是时空腰带，能瞬间将她传送回片刻之前，帮她脱离险境。

[DIALOGEVENT:MAIN_VINDICATOR]
Vindicators are close-support bombers effective against enemy vehicles.[3.8]They must reload at an Airbase between bombing runs.[7]They have a special ability that lets them quickly return to base to rearm and repair when needed.
维和轰炸机是近距离支援轰炸机，能有效对付敌方车辆。[3.8]每次轰炸任务之间必须返回空军基地重新装弹。[7]其特殊技能是必要时快速返回基地进行补给和维修。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_ARCHERMAIDEN]
Archer Maidens use their powered greatbows to strike enemy ground and air targets.[4.7]These bows can be extremely damaging in spite of being relatively slow on the draw.[9.5]The Maidens' special ability launches an energy arrow barrage that can strike multiple targets in an area.
弓箭少女使用她们的大威力弓矢攻击敌方地面与空中目标。[4.7]虽然拉弓射速相对较慢，但这些箭矢能够造成极大的杀伤。[9.5]弓箭少女的特殊能力是发射能量箭幕，可打击范围内多个目标。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_BURSTDRONE]
Burst Drones are flying robotic scouts used to survey enemy troop movements.[5]They may attach themselves to enemy vehicles, slowing the enemy down.[9.4]Burst Drones may also self-destruct, damaging nearby targets.
 爆裂机器人是飞行侦察机器人，用于侦察敌方部队动向。[5]它们可以附着在敌方车辆上，使其减速。[9.4]爆裂机器人也可自爆，对附近目标造成伤害。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_ENGINEER]
Engineers can instantly capture neutral or enemy structures, either on land or at sea.[5.2]They may also repair damaged structures.[7.8]Their special ability is to sprint.[10.7]After this brief burst of energy, they run out of breath and have to take a moment to recover.
工程师能立即占领陆地或海上的中立或敌方建筑。[5.2]他们也能修复受损建筑。[7.8]其特殊技能是冲刺。[10.7]在短暂爆发体力之后，他们会上气不接下气，需要花点时间恢复。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_GIGAFORTRESS]
Giga-Fortresses are among the most powerful weapons the Empire has ever developed.[4.7]In their primary form, their multiple cannons and photon rocket launchers are enough to overwhelm entire naval strike groups.[12.1]They may also switch to a secondary form, a devastating long-range artillery platform that can fly wherever it pleases.
超级要塞是帝国史上威力最强大的武器。[4.7]在主形态下，其多重火炮与光子火箭发射器足以压制整支海军打击群。[12.1]它们还可切换为另一种形态，化身为毁灭性的远程火炮平台，并能飞往任何想去的地方。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_KINGONI]
The King Oni are the Empire's heaviest ground forces.[3.3]These battlewalkers can incinerate foes with their eyeblasters.[7.8]Their special ability is the bull rush, which they use to close the distance with enemies,[12.5]or more often, to smash right into them and destroy them.
鬼王是帝国最强大的地面部队。这些机甲能用眼部能量炮将敌人烧成灰烬。[7.8]其特殊技能是野蛮冲撞，可用于拉近与敌人的距离，或直接冲撞并将其碾碎。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_MCV]
Mobile Construction Vehicles are amphibious self-powered bases that were reverse-engineered from Allied and Soviet counterparts.[7.8]They unpack into Construction Yards, which produce the nanocores that in turn convert to other Imperial structures.
移动基地车是两栖自供电基地，通过逆向工程从盟军和苏联同类装备发展而来。[7.8]它们可展开为建造基地，生产纳米核心，再由纳米核心转变为其他帝国建筑。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_MECHATENGU]
The Mecha Tengu are versatile fast-attack skirmishers whose twin autocannons are particularly deadly against infantry.[6.9]There is no terrain they cannot cross.[10]Their special ability is to transform into the Jet Tengu, a flying form effective against aircraft.
机甲天狗是多用途快速攻击部队，其双联机炮专门克制步兵。[5.4]它们能够穿越任何地形。[10]其特殊技能是变形为喷气天狗，一种能有效对付飞机的空中形态。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_MECHKING]
The Shogun Executioner is a one-of-a-kind weapon.[3]This colossal battle robot was created to instill panic and awe in the enemy while taking on entire armies.[9.6]It may engage multiple forces at once with its various weapons systems, and can crush almost anything under its massive feet.[16.9]It does require anti-aircraft support, however.
Value=将军刽子手是独一无二的武器。[3]这台巨大的战斗机器人既能对抗一整支军队，又能让敌人感到恐惧与敬畏。[9.6]它能利用多种武器系统同时对付多个目标，还能用巨大的脚掌碾碎几乎所有东西。[16.9]它需要防空部队的辅助。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_NAGINATACRUISER]
Naginata Cruisers are specially made to destroy enemy ships in a hail of torpedoes.[5.6]Their special ability launches a spread of auxilliary torpedoes from hidden launch bays.[10.6]This attack takes considerable time to rearm.
薙刀巡洋舰是专门用于发射大量鱼雷摧毁敌方舰艇的战舰。[5.6]其特殊技能是从隐藏发射管中射出呈扇面分布的备用鱼雷。[10.6]这种攻击需要相当长的装填时间。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_NANOCORE]
Nanocores are portable structures.[2.2]They can traverse land or sea, then unfurl into a full-sized structure where needed.[7.8]Because Cores can go almost anywhere, this provides Imperial bases with great flexibility.[13.4]Note that Cores cannot pack up once unfurled.
纳米核心是可移动的建筑结构。[2.2]它们能穿越陆地或海洋，在指定地点展开为功能完整的建筑。[7.8]由于核心几乎能抵达任何地方，这赋予了帝国基地极大的灵活性。[13.4]注意，核心一旦展开便无法收回。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_ORECOLLECTOR]
Ore Collectors are amphibious resource-gathering vehicles that travel between Ore Mines and Ore Refineries.[6.4]They must be protected at all times.[9.3]They can bring up a rapid-fire weapon to defend themselves as needed, although they cannot gather ore while this security system is activated.
采矿车是两栖资源采集车辆，在矿脉与矿石精炼厂之间往返。[6.4]必须时刻保护它们。[9.3]必要时，它们可以架起速射武器进行自卫，但此防卫系统启动期间无法采集矿石。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_ROCKETANGEL]
Rocket Angels are flying women warriors armed with photon rocket arrays, which are effective against armored targets both on the ground and in the air.[8.8]They may switch to using an energized Paralysis Whip, which stops a single target from moving or attacking.
火箭天使是搭载光子火箭阵列的飞行女战士，能有效对付地面和空中的装甲目标。[8.8]她们可切换为充能瘫痪鞭，阻止单个目标移动或攻击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_SEAWING]
Sea-Wings combine the functions of an anti-aircraft submarine with a close-air-support aircraft.[6.2]They must surface to attack in Sea-Wing form, whereas in Sky-Wing form, these units may attack relentlessly against ground or sea targets.
海翼结合了防空潜艇与近距离空中支援飞机的功能。[6.2]在海翼形态下，它们必须浮出水面才能攻击；而在天翼形态下，则可对地面或海上目标进行持续猛攻。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_SHINOBI]
Shinobi are expert infiltrator-assassins.[3]Their bladed weapons are lethal to infantry and they can traverse land and sea with equal ease.[9.1]Their special ability is a smoke bomb, which causes enemies to lose the Shinobi as a target.[14.4]If a Shinobi gets inside an enemy structure, he will disrupt the enemy in various ways depending on the structure.
忍者是精通渗透与暗杀的专家。[3]他们的刃类武器对步兵十分致命，且能同样轻松地穿越陆地与海洋。[9.1]其特殊技能是烟雾弹，可使敌人失去忍者的目标。[14.4]若忍者进入敌方建筑，他会根据建筑类型以不同方式干扰敌人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_SHOGUNBATTLESHIP]
Shogun Battleships are the finest vessels in the Imperial fleet.[3.8]Nothing can stand up to a barrage from their six main guns.[7.9]Their special ability provides a temporary burst of speed, allowing these ships to crash straight through other vessels.
将军战列舰是帝国舰队中最优秀的战舰。[3.8]没有任何目标能抵挡它们六门主炮的齐射。[7.9]其特殊技能是暂时加速，使这些战舰能直接撞穿其他船只。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_STEELRONIN]
The Steel Ronin are pilot-assisted robots built to withstand heavy fire in order to get within striking distance, where their polearms can slash through multiple targets in one stroke.[10.8]The Ronin's special ability sends a wave of energy coursing through the earth, damaging all in its path.
钢铁浪人是有人驾驶的机器人，能承受猛烈炮火以便进入攻击距离，再用长柄武器一击斩穿多个目标。[10.8]钢铁浪人还能沿地面释放毁灭性的冲击波，摧毁路径上的一切。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_STRIKERVX]
Striker-VXes are dual-form pilot-assisted robots designed to destroy enemy aircraft with their guided rockets.[6.6]But that is not all.[8.3]Their special ability is to transform into the flying Chopper-VX form, which is effective against armored targets on the ground.[15.5]Nothing is safe from these units.
打击者-VX是双形态有人驾驶机器人，设计用于以制导火箭摧毁敌方飞机。[6.6]它们的能力还不止于此。其特殊技能是变形为飞行的直升机-VX形态，能有效对付地面装甲目标。[15.5]没有目标能逃过这些单位的攻击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_SUDDENTRANSPORT]
Sudden Transports may disguise themselves as other vehicles to sneak Imperial infantry behind enemy lines.[6.7]Because they hover, they can cross land or sea equally as well.[11]Though unarmed with conventional weapons, Sudden Transports are rigged to explode if destroyed.
迅雷运输艇可以伪装成其他车辆，将帝国步兵秘密运送至敌后。[6.7]由于采用悬浮行进，它们能同样轻松地穿越陆地与海洋。[11]虽然没有配备常规武器，但迅雷运输艇被摧毁时会引发爆炸。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_TANKBUSTER]
Tankbusters are specially trained and outfitted to take on enemy armor.[3.9]Their personal plasma-cutter cannons can punch through a battle tank quickly.[8.7]Their special ability is to dive into a spider-hole, which boosts their defense and prevents them from being crushed by vehicles.[15.2]They can pop up to resume firing.
坦克杀手经过专门训练和装备，专门对付敌方装甲单位。[3.9]他们的单兵等离子切割炮能迅速击穿主战坦克。[8.7]其特殊技能是躲入散兵坑，既能提升防御力，又能防止被车辆碾压。[15.2]他们可以随时爬出散兵坑继续开火。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_TSUNAMITANK]
Tsunami Tanks are amphibious, fast-firing armored units that can quickly bring ruin to enemy bases and their military vehicles.[7.7]Their special ability briefly provides a boost to their defensive strength, though they cannot fire in this state.[13.6]They can still crush infantry under their treads, however.
海啸坦克是两栖速射装甲单位，能迅速摧毁敌方基地及其军用车辆。[7.7]其特殊技能是短时间内提升防御力，但此状态下无法开火。[13.6]不过，它们仍能用履带碾压步兵。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_WARRIOR]
Imperial Warriors fight using burst-firing rifles specially made to take down other infantry.[6.4]Their special ability gives them a window of opportunity to rush enemy infantry with beam katanas at the ready.[12.7]One stroke from these blades is lethal.
帝国武士使用专为击杀步兵而设计的连射步枪作战。[6.4]其特殊技能使他们能够趁机使用光束武士刀攻击敌方步兵。[12.7]这些刀剑一击毙命。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_WAVEFORCE]
Wave-Force Artillery are mobile bombardment vehicles that fire energy salvoes rather than basic ballistic warheads.[7.2]Their attack is extremely powerful though the firing sequence is slow.[10.9]However, their special ability lets them fire immediately--at the expense of some attack strength.
波能坦克是机动轰炸战车，发射能量弹幕而非传统弹道弹头。[7.2]其攻击威力极为强大，但发射流程缓慢。[10.9]其特殊技能可让其立即开火，但会牺牲部分攻击威力。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_YARIMINISUB]
Yari Mini-Subs are undersea scouts armed with tiny torpedoes effective against enemy naval forces.[6.6]These units must surface to fire but are invulnerable to most attacks while undersea.[11.4]Their special ability causes them to self-destruct into their targets.
长枪迷你潜艇是水下侦察单位，配备小型鱼雷，能有效对付敌方海军。[6.6]这些单位必须浮出水面才能开火，但在水下时能免疫大多数攻击。[11.4]其特殊技能是冲向目标并自爆。

[DIALOGEVENT:MIIN_YURIKO]
Yuriko Omega is our most powerful infantry unit.[3.3]She has been trained to use her mind as a weapon, and her mind can destroy almost anything.[9.3]She may pursue her enemies either on land or at sea.[12.6]Should she be outnumbered, she can send her foes flying using her Psychokinetic Burst special ability.
欧米伽百合子是我们最强的步兵单位。[3.3]她经过训练，能将心智化为武器，而她的意念几乎能摧毁一切。[9.3]她能在陆地或海上追击敌人。[12.6]若遭遇大量敌人围攻，她可使用超能波爆破特殊技能将敌人击飞。

Caution -- Sigma Harmonizer activated.[2.6]Battlefield control temporarily shutting down.
注意，西格玛协调器已启动。[2.6]战地控制暂时关闭。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_AKULASUB]
Akula Subs excel at quickly destroying enemy ships with their torpedoes.[5]They must surface to fire, but are invulnerable to most attacks while submerged.[10.3]Their special ability is to launch ultratorpedoes, which are devastating unguided warheads that rapidly accelerate straight ahead.
阿库拉潜艇擅长用鱼雷快速摧毁敌方舰船。[5]它们必须浮出水面才能开火，但下潜时能免疫大多数攻击。[10.3]其特殊技能是发射超级鱼雷，这是一种毁灭性的无制导鱼雷，能笔直加速前进。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_APOCALYPSETANK]
Apocalypse Tanks can easily take on other vehicles with their powerful main guns, and can crush most anything under their massive treads.[9.4]Their special ability is a magnetic harpoon, which can draw enemy vehicles in for the kill.
天启坦克能用威力强大的主炮轻松对付其他车辆，还能用巨大的履带碾碎大多数障碍物。[9.4]其特殊技能是磁力鱼叉，能将敌方车辆拖拽过来并加以击毁。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_BULLFROG]
Bullfrogs are amphibious anti-aircraft troop transports.[4.1]Their flak cannons can quickly destroy enemy aircraft.[8.4]The only means for passengers to exit a Bullfrog is via the "man cannon", which can be used to launch infantry behind enemy lines.
牛蛙战车是两栖防空运兵车。[4.1]其高射炮能快速摧毁敌方飞机。[8.4]乘员离开牛蛙的唯一方式是通过“人间大炮”，这种装置可将步兵发射到敌后。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_CONSCRIPT]
Conscripts are the Union's mainline anti-infantry forces.[4.4]They are not well-trained but can be effective in numbers.[8.4]They carry assault rifles and can also switch to using Molotov cocktails, which are more effective against structures, including garrisons.
征召兵是苏联的主力反步兵部队。[4.4]他们虽然训练不足，但凭借数量优势仍能发挥一定作用。[8.4]他们配备突击步枪，也可切换为燃烧瓶，对付建筑和驻防单位效果更佳。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_DESOLATOR]
Desolator Troopers are heavy infantry who fight using corrosive poisons stored in their chemical suits.[5.3]Contact with these poisons is almost instantly fatal to unprotected targets.[9.2]Desolator Troopers can either spray their toxins or launch them in explosive globs.
化学部队是重型步兵，使用储存于化学防护服内的腐蚀性毒液作战。[5.3]无防护的目标若接触这些毒液，几乎会立即致命。[9.2]化学部队可以喷洒毒素，也可将其以爆炸性液珠的形式发射。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_DESOLATOR1]
Desolator Troopers are heavy infantry who fight using deadly poisons stored in their chemical suits.[6]They can either spray their toxins on infantry or switch to their splattershot mode to launch explosive globs of corrosive acids against vehicles.[14.4]Their spray can also clear out enemy garrisons.
化学部队是重型步兵，使用储存于化学防护服内的致命毒液作战。[6]他们可向步兵喷洒毒素，也可切换至溅射模式，对载具发射爆炸性腐蚀酸液珠。[14.4]他们的喷洒还能清除敌方驻军。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_DREADNOUGHT]
Dreadnoughts are powerful long-range bombardment ships armed with multiple rocket launchers.[6]They can destroy any surface target in moments.[9.7]Their special ability makes them fire even more rapidly, although forcing the weapons systems to do this gradually causes internal damage.
无畏战列舰是强大的远程轰炸舰艇，搭载多座火箭发射器。[6]它们能在片刻间摧毁任何水面或地面目标。[9.7]其特殊技能可使其射速更快，但强行提升武器系统的射速会逐渐造成内部损伤。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_ENGINEER]
Combat Engineers can capture neutral or enemy structures both on land or at sea, or repair your damaged structures.[7.7]They carry basic revolvers for self-defense.[11.3]Their special ability is to build a Battle Bunker, which can be garrisoned by other infantry.
战斗工兵能占领陆地或海上的中立或敌方建筑，也能修复您受损的建筑。[7.7]他们随身携带基础左轮手枪用于自卫。[11.3]其特殊技能是建造一座战斗碉堡，可供其他步兵进驻。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_FLAKTROOPER]
Flak Troopers are convicts forced to carry out their sentence by fighting with heavy weapons on the front lines.[6.7]They are most effective against aircraft.[9.8]They can switch to using magnetic mines that work well against vehicles.[14.7]These mines will also detonate should a vehicle crush the Flak Trooper.
防空部队是由囚犯组成的部队，他们在前线使用重武器作战以抵偿刑期。[6.7]他们最擅长对付飞机。[9.8]他们还可以使用磁雷来对付车辆。[14.7]若有车辆碾压防空部队，这些地雷也会引爆。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_GRINDER]
Grinders are heavily-armored amphibious vehicles that lack conventional weapons but can simply roll over most smaller targets.[8]They can tear apart larger targets almost as quickly.[11.4]Their special ability gives them a temporary burst of speed, allowing them to overrun faster foes and deal more damage.
粉碎者是重型装甲两栖载具，虽未配备常规武器，但可碾碎绝大多数小型目标。[8]它们撕裂较大目标的速度同样惊人。[11.4]其特殊能力可提供短暂的爆发加速，使其能碾压更快的敌人并造成更多伤害。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_GRINDER1]
Grinders are heavily-armored amphibious vehicles that lack conventional weapons but can simply roll over most smaller targets.[7.5]They can tear apart larger targets almost as quickly.[10.9]Their special ability gives them a temporary burst of speed, allowing them to overrun faster foes.
粉碎者是重型装甲两栖载具，虽未配备常规武器，但可碾碎绝大多数小型目标。[7.5]它们撕裂较大目标的速度同样惊人。[10.9]其特殊能力可提供短暂的爆发加速，使其能碾压更快的敌人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_HAMMERTANK]
Hammer Tanks are effective against most vehicles and structures.[4.4]Further, they can crush infantry under their treads.[8.4]Instead of using their main gun, they can switch to using the Leech Beam, which absorbs enemy armor.[14.6]The beam can steal enemy weapons as well.
铁锤坦克能有效对付大多数车辆和建筑。[4.4]此外，它们能用履带碾压步兵。[8.4]除了主炮之外，它们还可切换为汲取光束，吸收敌方装甲。[14.6]这种光束同样能窃取敌方武器。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_KIROV]
Kirov Airships are flying heavy bombers.[3.1]Nothing on the ground can withstand their bombs.[6.1]Kirovs are slow but highly durable.[9.3]Their special ability provides them with a burst of speed using a chemical process that slowly causes them to take damage.[18.2]Therefore it must be used sparingly.
基洛夫飞艇是飞行重轰炸单位。[3.1]地面没有任何东西能抵挡它们的炸弹。[6.1]基洛夫速度缓慢但极为耐用。[9.3]其特殊技能通过化学方式提供爆发加速，但会逐渐损伤自身。[18.2]因此必须谨慎使用。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_MCV]
Mobile Construction Vehicles are amphibious mobile bases.[4.2]They are critical to battlefield operations, even though they have no weapons of their own.[9.6]Once unpacked into Construction Yards, they can build all facilities authorized by the Soviet war council.[16.6]They must be defended by any means necessary.
移动基地车是两栖移动基地。[4.2]它们虽然没有任何武装，但对战场行动至关重要。[9.6]一旦展开为建造基地，便能建造所有经苏联战争委员会授权的设施。[16.6]必须不惜一切代价保护它们。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_MIGFIGHTER]
MiG Fighters are specialized air-superiority jets, designed purely for intercepting groups of enemy aircraft.[7.9]They carry a limited supply of ammunition and must occasionally rearm at an Airbase.[13.6]They have a special ability to rapidly return to base if needed.
米格战斗机是专业的空优喷气战机，专为拦截敌方机群而设计。[7.9]它们携带的弹药有限，必须不时返回空军基地补充。[13.6]其特殊技能是在需要时快速返回基地。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_MORTARCYCLE]
Mortar Cycles are light artillery vehicles that excel at hit-and-run attacks versus enemy fortifications or slower forces.[7.8]They may quickly switch between using Molotov cocktails to burn their foes to a crisp, or raining down explosive shells with their mortar attack.
火炮机车是轻型炮击车辆，擅长以打带跑的战术对付敌方防御工事或速度较慢的部队。[7.8]它们能快速在投掷燃烧瓶烧焦敌人与发射迫击炮弹轰炸模式之间切换。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_NATASHA]
Natasha is the Union's greatest sniper-commando, whose rifle can kill several infantry in one shot.[7.2]She may also call in airstrikes against other targets.[11.5]Her special ability is to snipe pilots cleanly in their vehicles, such that the vehicles can then be captured.[18.8]Natasha is equally effective on land or at sea.
娜塔莎是苏联最优秀的狙击突击队员，她的步枪能一枪击杀数名步兵。[7.2]她还能呼叫空袭对付其他目标。[11.5]其特殊技能是精准狙杀车辆内的驾驶员，使这些车辆可以被占领。[18.8]无论是在陆地还是海上，娜塔莎都同样表现出色。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_ORECOLLECTOR]
Ore Collectors automatically gather resources essential to fueling the Soviet War Machine.[5.9]These amphibious vehicles must be protected at all costs.[9.9]Their special ability is to deploy reactive armor, which makes them highly resistant to enemy attacks.[17]Note they cannot gather resources in this state.
采矿车会自动采集必要的资源，以此来援助苏联战争机器。必须尽力保护这些两栖单位。它们的特殊技能可布署防护装甲，让它们可以顽强抵抗敌方的攻击。特别注意，它们在这个状态下无法采集资源。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_REAPER]
Reapers are bigger, tougher predecessors of the Sickle.[3.4]They are equipped with multiple grenade and rocket launchers, effective against multiple armored targets.[9.1]Their special ability is to jump, but their legs tend to break on impact.[13.7]As such, Reapers still serve well as fixed gun emplacements.
收割机甲是镰刀机甲的前身，体型更大、更坚固。[3.4]它们搭载了多重榴弹与导弹发射器，能有效对付多个装甲目标。[9.1]其特殊能力是跳跃，但腿部容易因冲击而断裂。[13.7]因此，收割机甲主要被用作固定炮台。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_REAPER1]
Reapers are bigger, tougher predecessors of the Sickle.[3.5]They are equipped with multiple grenade and rocket launchers, and are effective against multiple smaller targets, including aircraft.[10.7]Their special ability is to jump, but their legs tend to break on impact.[15.3]As such, Reapers still serve well as fixed gun emplacements.
收割机甲是镰刀机甲的前身，体型更大、更坚固。[3.5]它们搭载了多重榴弹与导弹发射器，能有效对付多个小型目标，包括飞机。[10.7]其特殊能力是跳跃，但腿部容易因冲击而断裂。[15.3]因此，收割机甲主要被用作固定炮台。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_SCIENTIST]
Soviet Chief Scientists have been plucked from the ranks of the Union's finest scientific minds and forced to work for the FutureTech Corporation against their will.[9.4]They are unfit for combat and must be protected.[12.6]However, they do have medical supplies, so they can heal friendly infantry.
苏联首席科学家是从联盟最顶尖的科学人才中选拔出来的，他们被迫违背自身意愿为未来科技公司效力。[9.4]他们不擅长战斗，必须加以保护。[12.6]但他们携带有医疗用品，因此可以治疗友方步兵。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_SICKLE]
Sickles are fast-moving anti-personnel vehicles armed with three independently-firing machine guns.[7.3]Their special ability lets them leap long distances, or even onto enemy infantry.
镰刀机甲是快速移动的反步兵单位，配备三门独立射击的机枪。[7.3]其特殊技能可使其长距离跳跃，甚至能直接跳向敌方步兵。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_SPUTNIK]
Sputniks are defenseless vehicles that offer an economical alternative to Mobile Construction Vehicles when it becomes necessary to expand.[9.6]These amphibious units may convert to an Outpost almost anywhere, allowing other Soviet structures to be built nearby.
史普尼克勘查车是无防御能力的车辆，在需要扩张时，可作为移动基地车的经济替代方案。[9.6]这种两栖单位几乎可在任何地方转换为前哨基地，以便在附近建造其他苏联建筑。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_STINGRAY]
Stingrays are experimental strike craft fitted with Tesla weapons that are effective against most targets.[6.7]Stingrays are amphibious, though slower-moving on land.[10.8]Their special ability is the Tesla Surge, which shoots lightning in all directions while briefly immobilizing the unit.[18]It only works on water.
磁爆快艇是实验性攻击艇，搭载了能有效对付大多数目标的磁爆武器。[6.7]磁爆快艇是两栖单位，但在陆地上移动较慢。[10.8]其特殊技能是磁暴电涌，朝各个方向释放闪电，同时短暂使自身无法移动。[18]此技能仅在水上有效。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_TERRORDRONE]
Terror Drones are amphibious robots that can kill enemy infantry or tear into vehicles and dismantle them from the inside.[8.2]Once infected, the vehicle must be repaired, or else it will be destroyed.[13.2]Terror Drones can switch to using an electro-stasis ray that shuts down vehicle locomotors, preventing them from moving.
恐怖机器人是两栖机器人，能击杀敌方步兵，或钻入车辆内部进行破坏。[8.2]一旦被入侵，该车辆必须进行修理，否则将被摧毁。[13.2]恐怖机器人可切换为电击停滞射线，关闭车辆的发动机，使其无法移动。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_TESLATANK]
Tesla Tanks are experimental vehicles whose dual Tesla coil weapons are more powerful than most any conventional weapon.[8.7]Like Tesla Troopers, Tesla Tanks may switch to using electromagnetic disrupters that disable enemy vehicles nearby.
磁爆坦克是实验性车辆，其双联磁暴线圈武器比大多数常规武器更为强大。[8.7]和磁爆部队一样，磁爆坦克可切换为电磁干扰装置，瘫痪附近的敌方车辆。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_TESLATROOPER]
Tesla Troopers are heavy infantry armed with electrifying weapons effective against most targets.[6.7]They are too big to be crushed by most tanks.[10.2]Their special ability is to switch to using electromagnetic disrupters that automatically disable enemy vehicles in the vicinity.
磁爆部队是重装步兵，配备能有效对付大多数目标的电击武器。[6.7]它们体型庞大，大多数坦克无法碾压。[10.2]其特殊技能是切换为电磁干扰装置，自动瘫痪附近的敌方车辆。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_TWINBLADE]
Twinblades are anti-surface combat helicopters armed with machine guns and rockets.[6.6]In addition to their powerful weapons, Twinblades function as transports, and can ferry infantry or some vehicles across the battlefield.
双刃直升机是配备机枪和导弹的对地攻击机。[6.6]除了强大的武器之外，双刃直升机还能作为运输工具，运送步兵或部分车辆穿越战场。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_V4ROCKETLNCHR]
V4 Rocket Launchers are long-range artillery pieces whose rockets can quickly destroy most any unit or structure, if they hit.[9.5]Their special ability is to fire multiwarheads that split up in flight, causing widespread damage.[16.3]Note V4s cannot defend themselves up close.
V4导弹发射车是远程火炮单位，其火箭弹若命中，可迅速摧毁大多数单位或建筑。[9.5]其特殊技能是发射飞行中会分散的多重弹头，造成大面积破坏。[16.3]注意V4不具备近距离自卫能力。

[DIALOGEVENT:MSIN_WARBEAR]
War Bears are trained for scouting by land and sea, as well as anti-infantry combat.[5.6]Their mauling attack is lethal against most infantry.[9.3]Their special ability is an amplified roar that causes paralysis in enemy infantry within earshot.[16.5]War Bears also detect disguised enemy units.
战熊受过陆地和海上侦察以及反步兵战斗训练。[5.6]它们的猛击对大多数步兵是致命的。[9.3]其特殊技能是增幅咆哮，能使可听见范围内的敌方步兵瘫痪。[16.5]战熊也能识破伪装的敌方单位。

了解电力不足的后果是很重要的。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT02_GUARDIAN18]

电力不足时，单位和建筑的建造速度会变慢，基地防御系统也会失效。因此在发展过程中保持充足电力始终是明智之举。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT03_GUARDIAN29]

*Structures can only repair as long as you have sufficient funds. You may notice that as your structure is repairing, your credits are slowly trickling down.
只有资金充足时才能修建筑。您会发现修的时候钱在慢慢往下掉。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER06]
*Top-Secret Protocols is just a fancy term for special support powers you can use. These will affect the battlefield, your enemies, and your units and structures.
最高机密协议说白了就是一些辅助技能，能用就对了。能影响战场、敌人、还有您自己的部队和建筑。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER07]
*Basically, they can get you out of a pinch.
简单说就是能救急。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER08]
*Keep in mind, you need your Construction Yard intact in order to use them.
记住，建造基地得活着才能用协议。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER09]
*As mentioned earlier, there are different types of Protocols.
刚才说过，协议分好几种。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER10]
*There are offense-oriented protocols such as Surgical Strike, which can damage enemy forces.
有攻击型的，比如精准打击，能直接伤敌。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER11]
*There are more-tactical protocols such as Chrono Rift, which can protect your units or split enemy forces.
有战术型的，比如超时空裂隙，能保护自己也能分割敌人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER12]
*And then there are upgrade protocols that provide passive bonuses, such as Advanced Aeronautics, which toughens up all those weak little Allied aircraft.
还有升级型的，提供被动加成，比如先进航空学，能给盟军那些小脆皮飞机加加硬度。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER13]
*Unlike offensive and tactical protocols, upgrade protocols take effect immediately once acquired and do not need to be activated manually.
跟攻击型和战术型不同，升级型协议到手就生效，不用手动激活。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER14]
*Next we'll talk about the Threat Meter, which gives you an impression of how much death and destruction is happening all around you in a given moment.
接下来聊聊威胁指示器，这玩意儿能告诉您当下周围打得有多热闹。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER15]
*The Threat Meter increases in battle, especially when you sustain heavy casualties, and the higher it is, the faster you earn new Security Points.
战斗中威胁指示器会往上涨，尤其是部队伤亡越惨重，涨得越快。涨得越高，安全点数来得也越快。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER16]
*Higher levels of threat also influence how quickly your units gain combat veterancy. The higher the threat, the faster your units will become great heroes from killing foes!
威胁等级高了，部队获取战斗经验的速度也更快。打得越凶，他们升到英雄级的速度就越快！

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER17]
*Veteran units display a little chevron. They're better at killing enemies, and once they achieve Heroic status, they automatically regenerate to boot.
精兵单位会带个小军衔标，杀敌更利索。升到英雄级之后还会自动回血。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER18]
*Remember, you also have access to your Top Secret Protocols to help you take down your enemy!
别忘了，您还能用最高机密协议来收拾敌人！

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_HAMMER19]
*Ow!! Whatever, figure out yourself then!
嗷！随便吧，自己想办法吧！

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI01]
*Each faction has its own set of Protocols, which can be offensive or defensive in nature--sometimes both.
每个阵营都有一套自己的协议，有的偏攻击，有的偏防御，也有的两者兼具。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI02]
*As you progress through a battle, you'll gradually earn Security Points, which you can use to access new Protocols.
战斗推进过程中，您会逐渐获得安全点数，用来解锁新的协议。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI03]
*Progress towards your next Security Point is indicated by the meter surrounding the Top-Secret Protocols button on your screen.
屏幕上最高机密协议按钮周围的那圈量表，显示的是您距离获得下一个安全点数还有多远。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI04]
*The number in the button is your current number of Security Points.
按钮上的数字就是您当前的安全点数。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI05]
*Progress towards your next Security Point is indicated by a special meter next to your Radar.
 雷达旁边有个专用量表，显示您距离下一个安全点数还有多远。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI06]
*The number beside the meter is your current number of Security Points.
量表旁边的数字就是您当前的安全点数。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI07]
*It appears you are under attack. Take advantage of your high threat meter and vet up your forces while using some of your Top-Secret Protocols to assist you.
看来您正在遭受攻击。趁威胁等级高的时候赶紧给部队升级，同时配合使用最高机密协议来助战。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT04_TSUNAMI08]
*Fear not, for this is only a simulation involving lifelike robots.
别担心，这只是一场模拟战，敌人都是栩栩如生的机器人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN01]
*Back for more, then, Commander? In this particular lesson, we’ll be going over some of the more-advanced play mechanics that'll separate the men from the boys.
又来上课了，指挥官？这一课我们要讲一些更进阶的玩法机制，能把高手和新手区分开的那种。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN02]
*From earlier lessons, you should be aware of the Allied Spy and some of his capabilities by now.
经过之前的课程，您现在应该对盟军间谍和他的本事有所了解了。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN03]
*Spies can disguise themselves as enemy units. Only scout units--the Allied Attack Dog, Soviet War Bear, and Imperial Burst Drone--can detect disguised enemy units.
间谍可以伪装成敌方单位。只有侦察单位：盟军警犬、苏联战熊和帝国爆裂机器人能识破伪装。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN04]
*Spies and Imperial Shinobi can also infiltrate enemy structures.
间谍和帝国忍者还可以渗透敌方建筑。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN05]
*When you infiltrate a structure, it'll impair your enemy's base in various ways, which should give you the upper hand.
渗透建筑后，会从不同方面削弱敌方基地，让您占据上风。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN06]
*For example, if you infiltrate their enemy Reactor, the entire base will power down for a while.
比如渗透敌方的反应堆，整座基地会停电一段时间。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN07]
*Go ahead and infiltrate the Instant Generator by right clicking on in it whilst your Spy is selected.
现在，选中您的间谍，右键点击瞬息发电机进行渗透。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN09]
*Good job, sir. Your units made it back and were able to whittle down those enemy tanks' health enough for your base defenses to finish them off.
干得好，长官。您的部队赶回来了，成功消耗了敌方坦克的血量，正好让基地防御清理它们。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN10]
*Had you issued a normal move order back to your base, your Guardian Tanks might not have fared as well.
要是当时只下了普通移动命令，守护者坦克未必能撑得住。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN11]
*Incidentally, should the enemy catch you off guard with an attack, listen for audio cues from your Intel Officer and visual cues on Radar to tip you off.
对了，要是敌人趁您不备搞偷袭，留意情报官的语音提示和雷达上的视觉信号。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN12]

要跳到最近的雷达事件位置，按空格键就行。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN13]

想从战场任何位置切回主基地，按H键。

*Ah, pants. It looks like the Empire is sending another attack wave, but this time they've come at us with infantry.
啊，麻烦了。帝国又派了一波攻势，这次来的是步兵。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN17]
*Your base defenses are strong against infantry, but with that many of the blighters, I don't think it's going to be enough.
基地防御设施能够对付步兵，但那么一大帮子涌过来，恐怕撑不住。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN18]

所以，收拾大批步兵的好办法，就是用战车碾压。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN19]

并非所有战车都能碾压，所以您最好记得哪些战车有这种能力。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN20]

像多功能步兵战车和激流ACV这种小型车辆，就是碾不了人的典型。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN21]

另一方面，像守护者坦克和突袭驱逐舰这类大型车辆，碾人就像碾碎薯片一样轻松。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN22]

此外，《红色警戒3》里一些最大的车辆，体型足以碾压其他更小的车辆。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN23]
*Anyhow - let's crush those robots!
那么，去碾压那些机器人吧！

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN24]

还以为您不会问呢！要碾压敌方步兵，选中您的守护者坦克，然后在敌方步兵后方下达移动命令。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN25]

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN27]

现在对您的坦克下达移动至指定位置的命令。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN28]
*Seeing as how that Rising Sun base seems intent on taking us down, let's go over some ways to get your units to protect your base without being drawn out by the enemy.
看来旭日帝国的基地似乎执意要消灭我们，我们来聊聊如何让您的部队保护基地，而不被敌人引诱出击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN29]
*All of your units can be set to different stances, which affect their behavior.
您所有单位都可以设置为不同的姿态，这会直接影响他们的行为。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN30]
*All units start off in Guard Stance, meaning they will auto-acquire enemies that come within their attack range, and pursue them for a short distance if they try to flee.
所有单位初始都处于警戒姿态，会主动攻击进入射程的敌人，若对方逃跑会短距离追击。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN31]
*Once the dust settles, your units will return to their original positions.
战斗结束，尘埃落定后，单位会返回最初位置。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN32]
*We need to have some of our units on defense for our base but not pursue our enemies.
我们需要一部分单位负责基地防守，但不追击敌人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN33]
*To do this, we need to put them into Hold Ground Stance. First, select one or more of your tanks.
要达成这一点，需要将他们设为固守姿态。首先，选中一辆或多辆坦克。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN34]
*Now click on the Advanced Commands panel in the bottom right of your screen next to the unit portrait.
现在点击屏幕右下角单位头像旁边的“高级命令”面板。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN35]
*As you can see, this panel reveals some additional controls for your units.
如您所见，这个面板列出了单位的一些额外控制选项。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN36]
*Now open the Command Menu and take a look at all the available stance options.
现在打开命令菜单，查看所有可选的姿态选项。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN37]
*Select the Hold Ground button to change your units' stance.
点击固守姿态按钮，切换部队姿态。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN38]
*Perfect. What say we also set up some base defenses? Each faction has a couple of its own base defenses. The Allies have Multigunner Turrets, for example.
很好。我们再布置一些基地防御如何？每个阵营都有自己的基地防御设施。比如盟军就有多功能炮塔。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN39]

点击侧边面板中的“支援建筑”标签页，建造一座多功能炮塔，放置在坦克附近。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN40]
*Open the Command Menu, navigate to your Support Structures queue, and build a Multigunner Turret. Then place it near your tanks.

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN41]

多功能炮塔在搭载不同步兵时会切换武器，不过默认使用的火箭发射器在大多数情况下已经够用。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN42]

顺便提醒您：如果选中部队后打开命令菜单，按B键可以直接返回顶层菜单。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN43]
*You don't need to deselect anybody.
您不需要取消选择任何单位。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN44]

如果想回到选中的单位，进入命令菜单中七点钟方向的“当前选中”按钮即可。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN45]
*Capital. Now if the Rising Sun attacks, we'll have some extra defenses waiting for them that they won't be able to draw away from out base. And here they come!
很好。现在旭日帝国如果来犯，我们会有一些额外防御单位等着他们，他们不会离开基地。现在敌人来了！

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_GUARDIAN46]

倒退移动的另一个好处是，让部队最厚实的正面装甲面对敌人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_HAMMER09]

多数车辆的后方和侧面装甲较薄弱，如果只用普通移动命令撤退，部队会掉头逃跑，承受更多伤害。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_HAMMER10]
*Reverse-moving allows you units' stronger front armor to take the brunt of the fire.
倒退移动可以让部队的前装甲始终面向敌人，从而扛住大部分火力。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK11]
*One further note about control groups: All units are placed into Control Groups automatically.
再补充一点关于编组的提示：所有单位都会自动归入编组。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK12]
*To access these, open the Command Menu, and select Control Groups in the 8 o'clock position. There you will always be able to select any of your units by type.
要访问这些分组，打开命令菜单，选择八点钟方向的编组。在那里您可以随时按类型选择单位。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK13]
*Now... please proceed with crushing my robots.
现在……请继续碾压我的机器人。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK14]
*Yes, the Empire's base defenses are quite impressive.
没错，帝国的基地防御相当扎实。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK15]
*You may need to use combined arms to defeat them. In fact, if you depend overmuch on just one or two types of units, you are bound to fail.
您可能需要多兵种协同才能击败它们。事实上，如果您过分依赖一两种单位，注定会失败。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK16]
*Thankfully, it is easy to manage multiple types of units at once.
幸运的是，同时管理多种单位并不困难。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK17]
*Try selecting all your combat forces on the battlefield using the Q key.
试着用Q键选择战场上所有战斗单位。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK18]
*One tap of Q selects all units onscreen. Two taps selects all units on the battlefield.
按一次Q选择屏幕上的所有单位，按两次选择战场上的所有单位。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK19]
*Good. Notice you have multiple unit types in the contextual tab in your Sidebar at right.
很好。请注意，右侧侧边面板的情景标签中有多种部队类型。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK20]
*One type of unit in your group will always be selected. To cycle between unit types, simply tap the Tab hotkey. Try this now.
您的编组中始终会选中一种单位类型。要切换类型，按Tab键即可。现在试试看。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK21]
*Yes, that's it. You may also cycle unit types in reverse order by pressing the Tab key while holding the Shift key.
对，就是这样。也可以按住Shift键的同时按Tab键，按相反方向切换。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK22]
*Try selecting all your combat forces on the battlefield by double-tapping the X button.
试着连续按两次X键，选择战场上所有战斗单位。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK23]
*Good. Notice that when you open the Command Menu, you have multiple unit types selected.
很好。注意，打开命令菜单时会看到已选中多种部队类型。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK26]
*You may navigate to different unit types in the Command Menu to use their special abilities or change their stances.
您可以在命令菜单中切换到不同的部队类型，使用特殊技能或切换姿态。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK27]
*Or, without opening the Command Menu, simply hold the Right Bumper while tapping up or down on the D-pad to cycle between unit types.
或者不打开命令菜单，直接按住右缓冲键的同时按方向键上或下，也能循环切换部队类型。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK28]
*Switching unit types in this fashion makes it easier to use your mixed group's special abilities, without deselecting the group.
用这种方式切换部队类型，可以更方便地使用混合编队的特殊技能，而且不需要取消编队。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK29]
*We should also briefly touch on Waypoint Mode, for it's useful. Waypoint Mode lets you queue up move or attack orders.
我们还需要简单讲讲路径点模式，它非常实用。路径点模式允许您按顺序排列移动或攻击命令。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK30]
*To use Waypoint Mode, hold the Alt key while issuing move or attack orders as normal with units selected. Your units will complete the orders in sequence.
要使用路径点模式，选中部队后按住Alt键，然后像平常一样下达移动或攻击命令。部队会按顺序依次执行。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK32]
*Now, earlier we had mentioned how directional armor comes into play during battles.
之前我们提到过，战斗中装甲是有方向性的。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK33]
*Another type of armor bonus your units can receive comes from an automatic reaction to enemy fire called suppression.
还有一种装甲加成来自一种名为“压制”的自动反应机制。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK34]
*Suppression only affects infantry units. When infantry units become suppressed, they go prone on the ground and continue firing.
压制只影响步兵单位。步兵被压制时会卧倒并继续开火。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK35]
*Suppressed infantry are slower but more resistant to damage.
被压制的步兵移动速度会变慢，但拥有更高的伤害抗性。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK36]
*Send your infantry into that city square there and attack that garrisoned structure.
派您的步兵进入那边那个城市广场，攻击那座驻军建筑。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK37]
*Banzi! As you approached the structure, your units came under enemy fire and some of them were suppressed as a result of the enemy attacks.
就是这样！当您靠近建筑时，部队遭到敌方攻击，其中一部分被压制了。

[DIALOGEVENT:MT05_TSUNAMITANK38]
*Due to this suppressed position, your units could absorb more damage and in turn deal more damage to that enemy structure, allowing them to destroy it.
在压制状态下，部队能承受更多伤害，同时也能对敌方建筑造成更多伤害，从而将其摧毁。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP01]
All commanders are advised to keep their bases fully powered at all times. Without enough power structures, your units and structures will build more slowly and your base defenses will cease functioning.
建议所有指挥官时刻保持基地电力充足。一旦电力不足，单位的训练和建筑的建造速度都会变慢，基地防御也会停止运作。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP02]
Use combined arms to achieve victory! A well-balanced combat group includes an assortment of anti-infantry, anti-vehicle, and anti-aircraft forces. Avoid relying too heavily on any one type of unit to prevent your enemies from easily countering your armies.
使用多兵种协同作战来赢得胜利！一支均衡的战斗编组，应包含反步兵、反车辆和防空部队。避免过度依赖单一类型的单位，否则敌人会轻易找到克制您的办法。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP03]
If your Construction Yard comes under heavy fire from enemy vehicles, it is advisable to escape to the waters (or onto land, as the case may be). Mobile Construction Vehicles are amphibious, and although they have no weapons, they are large enough to crush most enemies under their treads.
如果您的建造基地遭到敌方战车的猛烈攻击，建议收起基地转移到水面（或视情况从水面转移至陆地）。移动基地车具备两栖能力，虽然没有武装，但体型庞大，足以用履带碾碎大多数敌人。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP04]
Bombardment vehicles excel at taking down enemy base defenses. Should your enemy hunker down using advanced base defenses, roll in artillery to destroy them from long range! Better yet, take out the enemy's power structures to cause all those base defenses to shut down.
远程火炮单位擅长摧毁敌方基地防御。如果敌人用高级防御设施龟缩防守，就调来火炮在远距离上解决它们！更理想的做法是直接端掉敌人的发电设施，让所有防御系统瘫痪。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP05]
Fortress Walls, available in the Support Structures queue, can be used to slow enemy troop movements and prevent enemy Engineers, Infiltrators, and Commandos from getting into your structures.
围墙可在支援建筑队列中建造，用于减缓敌军推进速度，并阻止敌方工程师、渗透者与特种兵进入您的建筑。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP06]
If an enemy unit seems invincible, chances are there's a better way to fight it. Even the biggest, toughest combat vehicle has its weaknesses, and large groups of foes are all the more vulnerable to area-of-effect attacks. Mix up your tactics and keep the enemy guessing!
如果某个敌方单位看似无可阻挡，那多半是您没找对打法。即使是最庞大、最坚固的战车也有其弱点，大群敌人尤其容易受到范围攻击的伤害。请不断变换战术，让敌人捉摸不透！

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP07]
Your forces will not automatically attack enemy structures by default, in case you want to capture or infiltrate those structures. If you plan on laying waste to the enemy's base, try switching your forces to Aggressive Stance, or use the Assault-Move command.
默认状态下，您的部队不会主动攻击敌方建筑，这在需要占领或渗透这些建筑时非常有用。如果您打算把敌基地夷为平地，可以尝试将部队切换为侵略姿态，或使用行进攻击指令。

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP08]
Seasoned commanders agree: In battle, it's best to keep spending one's credits without exceeding one's income. This way you'll be building new military forces and additional structures at a good clip. Don't get caught sitting on thousands of credits with no army to defend them!
经验丰富的指挥官们都认同一条原则：战场上应保持支出不超过收入的速度持续花销，这样就能以稳定节奏生产部队和建筑。但千万别囤积大量资金，却没有军队守护这份家底！

[ECS:LOADSCREENGENERALTIP09]
If a strategy is ineffective, immediately discontinue its use! Avoid relying too heavily on any one tactic, lest your opponents catch on and bring the right counters. Keeping your enemies guessing is the key to victory.
如果某种策略无效，立即停止使用！避免过度依赖任何一种战术，否则敌人会识破您的意图并拿出针对性的反制手段。让敌人无法预测您的行动，才是取胜的关键。

超级要塞——除极少数帝国最高层领导人之外，无人知晓超级要塞的研发计划。这是一座庞大且拥有自我意识的武器平台。而真正了解其“神之鼻息”装置破坏力的人更是少之又少。

[ECS:LOADSCREENLISSETTEDESC]
COMMANDER LISSETTE HANLEY - As a former spy serving the Allied Special Forces, Lissette has deep insight into enemy strategies, as well as a one-of-a-kind knowledge of the Allies' most-sophisticated weapons. Her casual, almost-playful manner hints at years of experience in tense combat situations.
利塞特·汉莉指挥官——作为曾服役于盟军特种部队的间谍，利塞特对敌军战术有着深刻洞察，同时对盟军最尖端的武器也有着独到的了解。她那种随意、近乎俏皮的作风，恰恰体现了她在紧张战斗环境中多年磨练出的从容。

[ECS:LoadScreenLydia]
COMMANDER LYDIA WINTERS - Ms. Winters is considered to be the architect of the Allied orbital defense network, which was essential to the Allies’ victory in the last war. Having earned the right to take on new responsibilities, she pursued an opportunity to lead Allied forces directly, rather than keep watch from behind the curtain as before.
莉迪雅·温特斯指挥官——温特斯女士被认为是盟军轨道防御网络的设计者，该网络在上次战争的胜利中发挥了关键作用。在赢得了承担新职责的权利后，她抓住了直接领导盟军部队的机会，不再像以往那样在幕后观望。

[ECS:LoadScreenMortarCycle]
MORTAR CYCLE - Even the messenger-bikes once used for delivering telegrams, cakes, and toiletries were subsumed by the Soviet War Machine as fighting intensified. Once reinforced with light armor plating, aftermarket engines and handlebars, and light artillery weapons in the sidecar, these bikes proved to be very effective at fast assault.
随着战事加剧，就连曾经用来递送电报、蛋糕和日用品的摩托车也被卷入苏联战争机器。一旦加装轻型装甲、改装引擎和把手，并在边车装上轻型火炮，这些机车就成了快速突击中极其好用的利器。

[ECS:LoadScreenMoskvin]
COMMANDER NIKOLAI MOSKVIN - As the Union was thrown into chaos after the battle of Leningrad, this impetuous young commander, who was previously kept on a short leash due to concerns with his psychological disposition, quietly slipped away. He has lost none of his zeal for battle and still leads a small number of seasoned soldiers, who follow out of fear or respect.
尼可莱·莫斯克文指挥官——列宁格勒战役之后，苏联陷入一片混乱，这位因心理素质问题而长期被严加管束的鲁莽年轻指挥官悄然离去。他对战斗的热情丝毫未减，仍然率领少数几名经验丰富的士兵——那些人出于恐惧或尊敬而追随他。

[ECS:LOADSCREENMOSKVINDESC]
COMMANDER NIKOLAI MOSKVIN - This intense young man quickly rose through the ranks of the Red Army's largest Tesla Trooper regiment due to his unconventional yet brutally effective tactics, and now oversees the Union's experimental forces. Some say he is psychologically unstable... but never to his face.
尼可莱·莫斯克文指挥官——这位精力旺盛的年轻人，凭借不拘常规却又异常有效的战术，在红军最大的磁爆部队中迅速晋升，如今负责监督苏联的实验部队。有人说他心理不太稳定……但从没人敢当面对他说。

[ECS:LOADSCREENNAOMIDESC]
COMMANDER NAOMI SHIRADA - This gifted, no-nonsense fleet commander is heiress to a famous military family's legacy and is widely known to have little patience for those who get in her way of her ambitions. It is said she has no permanent home, preferring life aboard the ships in the armadas at her disposal.
白田直美指挥官——这位天赋异禀、行事果决的舰队指挥官，出身将门世家。众所周知，她绝不容忍任何阻碍自己野心的人。据说她没有固定的家，更喜欢住在自己麾下舰队的船上。

[ECS:LoadScreenOleg]
COMMANDER OLEG VODNIK - They say this veteran tank commander was tricked into accepting a lucrative-sounding promotion to Head of Soviet Military Affairs. After all, the Soviet military was nearly annihilated in the last war, so the Soviet people hungered to lay blame at someone's feet. Despite his unenviable position, Oleg maintains an unflappable attitude.
欧列格·沃德尼克指挥官——据说这位老练的坦克指挥官是被忽悠着接受了一个听起来很诱人的晋升——苏联军事事务负责人。毕竟，苏联在上次战争中几乎全军覆没，苏联人民迫切需要一个替罪羊。尽管身处如此不讨喜的位置，欧列格依然保持着一贯从容不迫的态度。

[ECS:LOADSCREENOLEGDESC]
COMMANDER OLEG VODNIK - Although casualty rates among Soviet conscripts are very high, opportunities for advancement abound for those who survive the front lines. Oleg is living proof of this. In spite of his informal demeanor, he has a proven ability to lead in battle and is well-liked by most of his men.
欧列格·沃德尼克指挥官——尽管苏联征召兵的伤亡率极高，但对于那些在前线幸存下来的人来说，晋升的机会比比皆是。欧列格就是活生生的例子。虽然他举止随意，但已在战斗中证明了自己的领导能力，并且深受大多数部下的爱戴。

[ECS:LoadScreenOmegaProgram]
THE OMEGA PROGRAM - The paramilitary firm Shiro Psychic Research secretly spent years developing an extremely ambitious system intended to augment the Empire of the Rising Sun's armed forces. Called the Omega Program, it required hundreds of human test subjects, which were brokered from orphanages, sanitariums, or wherever the unwanted gathered.
欧米伽计划——准军事机构神罗心灵研究中心秘密花费多年时间，开发一个野心勃勃的系统，旨在强化旭日帝国的武装力量，此即所谓的“欧米伽计划”。该计划需要数百名人类实验对象，这些人从孤儿院、疗养院，或其他收容“不受欢迎者”的地方搜罗而来。

[ECS:LoadScreenPacifier]
PACIFIER FAV - In order to keep their rivals firmly in check, the Allies commissioned an all-terrain artillery platform with unprecedented range as well as defensive systems to protect it at close range. FutureTech Corp. obliged with the Pacifier Field Artillery Vehicle, which quickly exceeded expectations.
平定者——为了牢牢压制对手，盟军委托研制一种全地形火炮平台，要求兼具超远射程和近程防御系统。未来科技公司应允并交付了平定者野战火炮载具，其表现迅速超出了预期。

[ECS:LoadScreenReaper]
REAPER - This not-entirely-successful old prototype from the Soviet Union's Ministry of Experimental Science somehow got the green light when the Union's situation continued to worsen and most military assets turned scarce. Its scores of rockets and grenades more than make up for its deficiencies.
收割机甲——这款来自苏联实验科学部、算不上完全成功的旧原型机，在苏联局势持续恶化、军事资源日益匮乏之际，竟意外获得了量产许可。其大量的火箭和榴弹载弹量，足以弥补自身的缺陷。

[ECS:LoadScreenShimada]
DR. SHINJI SHIMADA - As the managing director of the infamous Omega Program, Dr. Shimada is inadvertently responsible for some of the Empire of the Rising Sun's greatest weapons, such as the Psionic Decimator. He earned a Ph.D. from Tokyo's most prestigious science institute after his groundbreaking (though controversial) thesis on latent psionics and the next stage of human evolution.
岛田真司博士——作为恶名昭彰的欧米伽计划的主持人，岛田博士在无意中负责了旭日帝国一些最强大武器的研发，例如超能波毁灭装置。他在发表了关于潜在超能力与人类进化下一阶段的开创性（尽管备受争议）论文后，获得了东京最负盛名的科学研究所的博士学位。

[ECS:LoadScreenShinzo]
COMMANDER SHINZO NAGAMA - The untimely death of Emperor Yoshiro took a heavy toll on Shinzo, who was considered one of the late Emperor's most-trusted retainers. Shinzo returned to a life of seclusion at his ancestral home in Shin Iga Province, where he is believed to be searching for spiritual guidance. A less-popular theory is that he is plotting to fulfill the Emperor’s final wishes.
长间晋三指挥官——天皇芳朗意外地撒手西归，对晋三造成了沉重打击，他是已故天皇最信赖的家臣之一。晋三回到位于伊贺国的祖宅，过起了隐居生活，据传他正在那里寻求精神指引。也有一种不那么普遍的说法认为，他正密谋实现天皇的临终遗愿。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSHINZODESC]
COMMANDER SHINZO NAGAMA - A calculating military leader who comes from a long line of Imperial military advisors seeking the Empire's rise to absolute power. Shinzo was one of the architects of the Imperial invasion of the Soviet Union, and has taken it upon himself to train a new generation of commanders.
长间晋三指挥官——他是一位很擅长战略的军事领导者，他的先人也都是帝国的顾问，而他的最终目标就是如何让帝国成为最强大军事国度。晋三也是策划侵略苏联的其中一名顾问，他也因此训练了一批新一代的指挥官。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETAIRFIELDDESC]
The Soviet airforce has grown bold in recent years. Once limited to producing smaller craft, airfields are now retrofitted to manufacture everything from the sleek MiG Fighters to the massive Kirov Airships. Today, Soviet air superiority arguably rivals that of the Allied nations.
 苏联空军近年来愈发强势。过去，空军基地仅限于生产小型飞机，如今经过改造，已能制造从流线型的米格战斗机到庞大的基洛夫飞艇在内的所有机型。如今，苏联的空中优势可以说已经能与盟军匹敌。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETAKULADESC]
AKULA SUB - Invisible to enemy radar while submerged, Soviet Akula Subs lurk undersea in search of enemy ships. Although they must surface to fire, they are powerful enough to assault larger vessels directly.
阿库拉潜艇——苏联阿库拉潜艇在水下潜航时可躲避敌方雷达探测，潜伏于深海搜寻敌方舰船。虽然它们必须浮出水面才能开火，但其火力足以直接攻击大型舰艇。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETCHERDENKODESC]
PREMIER ANATOLY CHERDENKO - Longtime leader of the Soviet Union, who has led the Union in an age of prosperity and military superiority. Once a famous theoretician, he rose to power meteorically even as Soviet forces under his advisement achieved victory after victory against their enemies the Allies.
安纳托利·查丹科总理——苏联的长期领导人，曾带领苏联步入繁荣与军事强盛的时代。他曾是著名的理论家，在其建议和指导下，苏联军队对盟军取得了一场又一场的胜利，而他也随之迅速崛起，飞黄腾达。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETDASHADESC]
INFORMATION OFFICER DASHA FEDOROVICH - Born to a poor family whose 10 children all willingly enlisted in the Soviet armed forces, Dasha was singled out as the most talented of the lot, so much so that Premier Cherdenko himself took notice of her. She now serves as an essential conduit between Soviet commanders across multiple fronts.
达莎·费多洛维琪情报官——出身贫寒，家中十个孩子都自愿投身苏联军队，达莎是其中最出色的一个，连查丹科总理都亲自注意到了她。如今她担任多条战线上苏联指挥官之间的重要信息渠道。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETIRONCURTAINDESC]
IRON CURTAIN - The Iron Curtain is one of the Soviet military's most-feared creations. It gathers some sort of energy inside its furnace, which it uses to project a kinetic barrier onto armored forces. While the barrier lasts only a short while, affected targets are completely invulnerable during that time.
铁幕——铁幕是苏军最恐怖的发明之一。它在熔炉中聚集某种能量，将其投射到装甲部队上形成动能屏障。屏障效果虽然短暂，但受影响的目标在此期间完全免疫所有伤害。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETKRUKOVDESC]
GENERAL BORIS KRUKOV - Having fought and won countless battles against Allied forces, Krukov is a war hero in the eyes of the Soviet people. Those who know him personally, however, are more likely to be deathly afraid of getting on his bad side. He never could have achieved his status without being strict and without being ruthless.
库可夫将军——库可夫在与盟军的战斗中屡战屡胜，在苏联人民眼中是战争英雄。然而，那些真正了解他的人，大多十分惧怕惹恼他。若非他的严苛与冷酷，他绝不可能有今天的地位。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETNATASHA01DESC]
NATASHA VOLKOVA - Few know the true character of Natasha Volkova, the Union's legendary sniper, though her name and exploits are known across the world. A woman of few words, she is a seasoned professional--and, some would say, a cold-blooded killer. She deals with only the most urgent matters affecting the Union.
娜塔莎·沃尔科娃——苏联的传奇狙击手，她的名字和事迹闻名于世，但很少有人了解她的真实性格。她沉默寡言，是经验丰富的专业人士，也有人说她是个冷血杀手。她只处理关乎苏联存亡的最紧急事务。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETNATASHA02DESC]
NATASHA - So famous is this Soviet sniper-commando that her likeness is heavily used for purposes of military recruitment and propaganda. Some say she is hell-bent on living up to her living-legend image. She is extremely skilled with her custom rifle and has full authority to call airstrikes.
娜塔莎——这位苏联狙击突击队员的名气之大，使她的形象被广泛用于征兵和宣传活动。有人说她一心想要不辜负自己“活着的传奇”这一称号。她熟练地使用自己的定制步枪，并拥有直接呼叫空袭的全权。

[ECS:LoadScreenSoviets1]
The Soviet Union slid into a state of deep turmoil and disorder after the Allied defeated its leaders at Leningrad. However, because the Allies were spread thin trying to demilitarize the Empire of the Rising Sun at the same time, a few brave and well-connected Soviet patriots quickly seized the opportunity to form a resistance movement, with intent to restore the Union to greatness.
在盟军于列宁格勒击败苏联领导人之后，苏联陷入了深重的动荡与混乱之中。然而，由于盟军同时还要解除旭日帝国的武装，兵力捉襟见肘，少数勇敢且关系友好的苏联爱国者迅速抓住机会，组建了抵抗运动，意图让苏联重归往日的荣光。

[ECS:LoadScreenSoviets2]
In testament to the pride and determination of the Soviet spirit, the surviving forces of the Union's military soon rallied after the defeat of their corrupt leaders at Leningrad, in spite of countless losses and setbacks. Although the Soviet military now is but a shadow of its former self, some of its newest and most talented commanders are working tirelessly to carry their nation forward.
尽管遭遇了无数损失与挫折，在那些腐败领导人于列宁格勒败亡之后，苏联军队的残存力量很快重新集结，以此证明苏联精神的骄傲与坚定。尽管如今的苏联已远不如昔，但一些新任的杰出指挥官正在不懈努力，引领他们的国家继续前行。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETTESLACOILDESC]
TESLA COIL - The modern Soviet Tesla Coil is far different from the innocuous scientific device from which it takes its name. As one of the deadliest weapons developed at the Ministry of Experimental Science, this device can reduce a full-grown man to mere ash in just one tremendous electrical jolt.
磁爆线圈——现代苏联的磁爆线圈，与其同名的无害科学装置截然不同。作为实验科学部开发的最致命武器之一，它只需一次强力电击，就能将一名成人化为灰烬。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETTHREEAPOCSDESC]
The Soviets are virtually unchallenged on the ground, due to their exceptionally powerful and sturdy armor divisions. If only their Apocalypse Tanks could swim, say some Soviet political pundits, then surely the Union would have taken over all the world by now.
苏联在地面上几乎无人能敌，这得益于其异常强大且坚固的装甲部队。一些苏联政治评论家说，要是天启坦克能下水，苏联早就统治全世界了。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETZELINSKYDESC]
DOCTOR GREGOR ZELINSKY - An ingenious and well-to-do scientist responsible for many popular Soviet household goods, a majority of which were appropriated by the Ministry of Experimental Science and eventually turned into weapons. A peaceful man by nature, Zelinsky is ambivalent about his current status as Union's top military scientist.
格雷戈尔·泽林斯基博士——一位才华横溢且家境殷实的科学家，曾负责研发许多广受欢迎的苏联家电，然而其中大部分被实验科学部征用并最终改造成了武器。泽林斯基天性爱好和平，对如今身为苏联顶级军事科学家的身份感到矛盾。

[ECS:LOADSCREENSOVIETZHANA2DESC]
Faced with a new threat in the Empire of the Rising Sun, the Soviet military's publicity department worked tirelessly to produce high-impact propaganda with which to compel millions of young men into enlisting willingly. Pictured: Commander Zhana Agonskaya, posing for a Red Air Force recruitment campaign.
面对旭日帝国这一新威胁，苏联军方的宣传部门不遗余力地制作强有力的宣传材料，以促使数百万年轻人自愿入伍。图为：夏娜·阿肯斯雅指挥官为红军空军征兵活动拍摄的宣传照。

[ECS:LoadScreenSteelRonin]
STEEL RONIN - Most Imperial soldiers found guilty of treason or war crimes are promptly executed. The most experienced ones, however, instead are locked in a sort of solitary confinement within the massive frame of a Steel Ronin assault suit. There they would lie for years in a fugue state, to be awakened for battle only in the event of the Empire's darkest hour.
钢铁浪人——大多数犯下叛国或战争罪的帝国士兵会被直接处决。但那些经验丰富的老手，则会被关进钢铁浪人突击装甲里，相当于一种活着的监禁。他们在里面常年处于恍惚状态，只有帝国到了最危急的关头，才会被唤醒参战。

[ECS:LoadScreenTakara]
COMMANDER TAKARA SATO - After the Allies’ victory over the Empire, the former chief of the Rocket Angels' First Battalion quickly rose to become a strong leader in the newly-founded Imperial Final Defense Force. Takara's experience in surprise assaults has made her an elusive target, and she prides herself on being an increasingly-painful thorn in the Allies' side.
佐藤高良指挥官——盟军战胜帝国后，这位前火箭天使第一营的指挥官迅速崛起，成为新成立的帝国终极防卫部队中一位有力的领导者。高良在突袭作战方面经验老道，让盟军很难抓到她，而她也以自己能不断给盟军制造麻烦为傲。

[ECS:LoadScreenTatsu]
CROWN PRINCE TATSU - After the death of his father, the Empire's citizens feared for his only son. But rather than fall prey to desperation or despair, Prince Tatsu did something no one, not even the Allies, expected: He surrendered, and agreed to cooperate with the Allies to put an end to their war. The Imperial people perceived this surprising move as both bold and cowardly in equal measure.
达郎王子——天皇驾崩后，帝国百姓都为这位唯一的皇子捏了一把汗。然而达郎皇王子既没有绝望也没有赌上一切，而是做了一件谁都没料到的事——他投降了，同意与盟军合作来结束战争。帝国上下对这一惊人之举褒贬不一，有人认为他够胆识，也有人觉得他懦弱。

[ECS:LoadScreenThornley]
PRES. RUPERT THORNLEY - The President of the European Union is a shrewd ambassador and businessman, who worked tirelessly behind the scenes during the last war in order to secure the necessary funding and support that eventually enabled the Allies to prevail. In the aftermath of the war, he endeavored to publicize these successes.
总理鲁伯特·索恩利——这位欧盟领导人既是精明的外交家，也是老练的生意人。上次战争期间，他在幕后四处奔波，为盟军最终获胜争取到了必不可少的资金与支持。战后，他又不遗余力地把这些功劳宣扬出去。

[ECS:LoadScreenVera]
COMMANDER VERA BELOVA - Known as the White Sparrow, this Ukrainian freedom fighter restored some sense of order and hope to the Soviet people after the collapse of their leadership after the war. Her promises of glory and retribution have inspired a new generation of Soviets to take up arms in the defense of their nation.
维拉·贝洛瓦指挥官——人称“白麻雀”，这位乌克兰自由斗士在战后苏联领导层崩溃、社会陷入真空的时期，为苏联人民重新带来了秩序与希望。她对荣耀与复仇的许诺，激励了新一代苏联人拿起武器，保卫自己的祖国。

[ECS:LOADSCREENWARRENDESC]
COMMANDER WARREN FULLER - Not one for mincing words, Warren has survived numerous battles against the Soviets through regimented training, determination, and tenacity. Once captured by the Soviets, he fought his way to freedom with his bare hands. His men know he has a score to settle.
温伦·富勒指挥官——他向来直言不讳，凭借严格的训练、决心和坚韧，在多场对苏战斗中幸存下来。他曾被苏军俘获，但空手杀出重围重获自由。他的部下都知道，他有一笔账要算。

[ECS:LOADSCREENZHANADESC]
COMMANDER ZHANA AGONSKAYA - Men and women alike are conscripted into the Red Army, and Zhana distinguished herself almost immediately from among all the millions drafted as a brilliant pilot, good leader, and proud patriot. Now many of the Union's top aces seek transfer to her command.
夏娜·阿肯斯雅指挥官——红军征兵不分男女，而夏娜在数百万应征者中脱颖而出。她既是出色的飞行员，又是优秀的领导者，更是一位自豪的爱国者。如今，许多苏联顶尖王牌飞行员都希望调至她的麾下。

[MISSIONOBJ:A01_BONUSOBJECTIVE_4_DESC]
The Soviets are bringing in a naval force, which Peacekeepers will be ill-equipped to fend off. Train at least 5 Javelin Soldiers from your Boot Camp to help counter the Soviet navy.
苏联正在调遣海军部队，维和步兵的装备不足以抵挡他们。从训练营训练至少五名标枪兵，协助对抗苏联海军。

[MISSIONOBJ:A01_BONUSOBJECTIVE_5_DESC]
You are authorized to build Multigunner IFVs at the Armor Facility. Build at least 5 IFVs before the Soviets arrive, to help fend them off.
您已获批在装甲工厂建造多功能步兵战车。在苏联军队抵达前建造至少五辆，以协助抵挡他们。

按下“F”键，然后左键单击空旷地形展开您的移动基地车。接着点击相应的建造按钮，建造一座发电厂、一座矿石精炼厂和一座新兵训练营。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_02_DESC]
Select your Prospector and left-click its special ability button. Then left-click on open terrain to unpack it. Try unpacking across the water on the small island.
选择您的采矿车，左键单击其特殊技能按钮，然后左键单击空旷地形将其展开。尝试在河对面小岛的地形上展开。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_02_DESC_360]
Select your Prospector, hold the Right Bumper, and press the X button to use its Unpack ability. Then press the A button on open terrain to unpack it. Try unpacking across the water on the small island.
选择您的采矿车，按住右肩键并按下X键以使用其展开能力。然后在空旷地形上按下A键将其展开。尝试在河对面小岛的地形上展开。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_03_DESC]
Build a Reactor and a Crusher Crane by clicking on their respective buttons, then clicking on open terrain.
点击相应的建造按钮，然后点击空旷地形来建造一座反应堆和一座压碎起重机。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_04_DESC]
Construct a Generator Core by clicking on its button in the Production Structures tab. When the Generator Core emerges from the Construction Yard, select it and left-click on its Unfurl ability button. Then left-click on open terrain.
点击生产建筑标签中的发电机核心按钮来建造一个发电机核心。当发电机核心从建造基地中出来后，选中它，左键单击其展开能力按钮，然后左键单击空旷地形。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_05_DESC]
Upgrade both your Construction Yard and Command Hub by selecting them and left-clicking on the Heightened Clearance buttons in the Sidebar.
选择您的建造基地和指挥中心，然后在侧边栏中左键单击更高权限按钮进行升级。

[MISSIONOBJ:T02_OBJECTIVE_05_DESC_360]
Upgrade both your Construction Yard and Command Hub by selecting them, opening the Command Menu, and selecting the Heightened Clearance button for each structure.
选择您的建造基地和指挥中心，打开指令菜单，然后为每座建筑选择更高权限按钮进行升级。

[MISSIONOBJ:T04_OBJECTIVE2_DESC]
Use the Surveillance Sweep protocol to sweep across a section of the battlefield by selecting the power, then left-clicking on the map at two locations.
使用侦察扫描协议扫描一片战场区域。先选中该技能，然后在地图上左键点击两个位置。

Issue a Take Position Directive Here
在此处下达占领据点指令

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE02]
Issue a Strike Target Directive Here
在此处下达攻击目标指令

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE03]
Issue a Plan Attack Directive Here
在此处下达计划攻击指令

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE04]
Issue the Keep Command Directive to Your Co-Commander
向您的合作指挥官下达继续指挥指令

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE05]
Execute the Co-Commander Strike
执行合作指挥官出击

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE01_DESC]
To issue a Take Position directive, left-click on the Take Position button in the Co-Commander Monitor. Then left-click on the terrain.
要下达占领据点指令，左键单击合作指挥官监控面板中的占领阵地按钮，然后左键单击地形。

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE02_DESC]
To issue a Strike Target directive, left-click on the Strike Target button in the Co-Commander Monitor. Then left-click on an enemy unit or structure.
要下达攻击目标指令，左键单击合作指挥官监控面板中的攻击目标按钮，然后左键单击敌方单位或建筑。

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE03_DESC]
To issue a Plan Attack directive, left-click on the Plan Attack button in the Co-Commander Monitor. Then left-click on the terrain. You may also left-click on an enemy.
要下达计划攻击指令，左键单击合作指挥官监控面板中的计划攻击按钮，然后左键单击地形（也可左键单击敌人）。

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE04_DESC]
To issue a Keep Command order, left click on the Keep Command button in the Co-Commander Monitor.
要下达继续指挥指令，左键单击合作指挥官监控面板中的继续指挥按钮。

[MISSIONOBJ:T06_OBJECTIVE05_DESC]
To execute the Co-Commander Strike, left-click on the Co-Commander Strike beacon on the battlefield. Then left-click on the Execute button.
要执行合作指挥官出击，左键单击战场上的合作指挥官打击信标，然后左键单击执行按钮。

[MISSIONOBJ:TO3_OBJECTIVE2_DESC]
Garrison and Evacuate Peacekeepers using the special ability buttons on a civilian structure, Multigunner IFV, and Riptide ACV.
使用民用建筑、多功能步兵战车和激流ACV上的特殊技能按钮，让维和步兵进驻或撤出。

To pan the camera, hold down the right mouse button and move your mouse around. To rotate the camera, hold down the middle mouse button and move the mouse around. To zoom the camera move your mouse wheel back and forth.
按住鼠标右键并拖动可平移视角；按住鼠标中键并拖动可旋转视角；滚动鼠标滚轮可缩放视角。

[MISSIONOBJ:TUTORIAL_OBJECTIVE4_DESC]

[OBJDESC:CAPTURE_VIP_BUNKERS]
Send Engineers into the Soviet V.I.P. Bunkers to transfer all Soviet structures in the area to your control. Force their obedience!
派遣工兵进入苏联高级指挥官碉堡，将区域内所有苏联建筑的控制权转交给您。用武力迫使他们服从！

[OBJDESC:DESTROY_TIME_MACHINE]
Hurry and destroy the Kremlin and the Time Machine before the Premier escapes with it!
在总理利用时间机器逃走之前，赶紧摧毁克里姆林宫和时间机器！

[OBJDESC:J02_BONUS_STATUES]
The Emperor expects for all Soviet cultural monuments to be brought down, in order to break the spirits of the Soviet people.
天皇要求摧毁所有苏联纪念碑，以瓦解苏联人民的精神意志。

[OBJDESC:J02_ESTABLISH_BEACHEAD]
Escort the Imperial construction vehicles to the staging area, then assemble a military base with which to lay claim to this land.
护送帝国的基地车抵达集结区域，然后建立一座军事基地，以此宣告对这片土地的占领。

[OBJDESC:J02_KILL_STATUE]
The Mother Russia Statue is one of the Union's most famous landmarks, symbolizing the Soviets' assumed military might and prosperity. Topple it to the ground.
苏联母亲雕像是苏联最著名的地标之一，象征着他们所谓的军事力量与繁荣。将它推倒在地。

[OBJDESC:J02_TRANSPORTS]
Cargo transports bearing secret equipment will arrive via the river to the east and travel westward. Ensure their survival.
运载秘密装备的运输货柜将从东面河道抵达并西行。确保它们安全到达。

[OBJDESC:J03_ARTILLERY]

使用强大的将军刽子手粉碎苏联炮台阵地。

Demoralize the Soviet troops even further by destroying their beloved landmarks here.
摧毁此处他们珍视的地标，进一步挫伤苏联军队的士气。

[OBJDESC:J03_DESTROY_KRUKOV]
General Krukov is attacking you from one of the northern bases.  Destroy them both, just to be sure.
库可夫将军正在从其中一座北方基地向您发起进攻。为了保险起见，把两座基地都摧毁。

[OBJDESC:J03_ESCORT_PARTS]
Protect General Tatsu's cargo transports on their route to the assembly area. When all the parts have arrived, the secret weapon will be assembled.

[OBJDESC:J07_DEFEND_OUTPOSTS]

援军正在途中。阻止盟军压制您的前哨基地，并准备反击。
