[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_01]
The Allies' notorious Attack Dogs are bred for battle near this training camp, run by Commander Hill, a washed-up old war hero who hasn't seen frontline battle in more than a decade. That is to say, his bark is worse than his bite. So how about we have a look at what he's up to? Just keep in mind that while he's likely to be a pushover this time around, he'll probably remember some of his old tricks when next you meet.
盟军臭名昭著的警犬就在这个训练营附近培育，希尔指挥官负责管理该营。希尔是个过气的战争英雄，已经十多年没有上过前线了。说白了，他就是个雷声大雨点小的家伙。所以，您何不去瞧瞧他到底在搞什么名堂？不过要记住，虽然这次他很可能是个软柿子，但下次再碰面时，他或许会拿出老把戏来对付您。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_02]
In the Empire of the Rising Sun, battlefield commanders settle their disputes and trade their honor by conducting elaborate wargames, such as the one you are about to interrupt. After all, we could use a better understanding of Imperial tactics and military traditions. Together, your opponents here may pose a threat, but their tradition dictates that such duels must never be interrupted. So why not let the venerable Commander Shinzo and his wayward protégé Kenji wear each other down before you send them both packing?
在旭日帝国中，战场指挥官通过精心设计的军事演习来解决纷争、较量荣誉——而您即将介入的正是这样一场演习。毕竟，我们正需要更深入地了解帝国的战术与军事传统。单独来看，您面前的对手或许不足为惧，但他们联手时仍能构成威胁——不过依照他们的传统，这类对决绝不容外人插手。所以，您何不让德高望重的晋三指挥官和他那桀骜不驯的弟子贤治先互相消耗，等他们两败俱伤再一并收拾掉呢？

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_03]
Commanders from each of the world powers are converging on this resource-rich archipelago in order to wrest control of the numerous Oil Derricks. They will be fighting fiercely amongst themselves, which should create the perfect opportunity for your naval forces to swoop in and finish them all. Think of the research-and-development we could green-light with all those Oil Derricks!
世界各大势力的指挥官正齐聚这片资源丰富的群岛，为争夺大量钻油塔的控制权而明争暗斗。他们之间势必会打得不可开交，这恰好为您提供了绝佳的机会，让您的海军部队趁势出击，将他们一网打尽。想想看，有了那些钻油塔，我们能批准多少研发项目！

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_04]
The Rising Sun's ever-enthusiastic Commander Takara is running maintenance on one of those gigantic floating fortresses, due to a software malfunction in the targeting systems of its Wave-Force Tricannons. This is the perfect opportunity to learn more about those fortresses, as we can get some your forces onto the fortress and rig some of the Tricannons to defend your position. Even still, approaching Takara's Tricannons by land would be tantamount to suicide. Stick to long-range naval bombardment or airstrikes.
旭日帝国那位总是热情高涨的高良指挥官，正在维修其中一座巨大的浮岛要塞——因为它的波能三重炮瞄准系统出了软件故障。这正是深入了解这些要塞的绝佳时机，我们可以把您的一部分部队送上要塞，并操控部分三重炮来协助您防御。不过，从陆地靠近高良的三重炮无异于自杀。还是坚持用远程舰炮轰炸或空袭吧。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_05]
The Imperial King Oni may not look like an obvious match for a main battle tank, but has more than proven itself in battle. Commander Kenji happens to be training a bunch of them here to smash through things with even greater efficiency. We would dearly love to understand what makes the Onis tick--we're not even sure if there's a flesh-and-blood pilot inside of those things. Why don't you spoil Kenji's little exercise so we can find out?
帝国的鬼王乍看之下并非主力坦克的对手，但它早已在实战中充分证明了自己。指挥官贤治恰巧正在这里训练一批鬼王，让它们更高效地进行冲撞破坏。我们非常想弄清楚鬼王的运作机理——我们甚至不知道那厚重的装甲里面，是否有一个血肉之躯的驾驶员。您何不去搅乱贤治的小规模训练，好让我们一探究竟呢？

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_06]
One of our key research centers and resorts is in trouble, as two of the most reckless commanders you've fought thus far have decided to show up for a little off-the-record payback. With any luck, the joke will be on good commanders Kenji and Moskvin: You will have immediate access to all three types of construction yards and plenty of resources with which to take them on.
我们的一处重要研究中心兼度假胜地正陷入麻烦——您迄今为止交过手的两位最鲁莽的指挥官，决定跑来搞一次私下报复。如果运气够好，贤治和莫斯克文这两位“优秀的指挥官”会吃不了兜着走。您将立即获得所有三种建筑工厂的使用权，以及充足的资源来对付他们。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_07]
More spacecraft have crashed on this island than in all science-fiction feature films combined, so we could definitely use it. We strongly advise you to keep moving while engaging the Soviet resistance movement's favorite sweetheart Vera in this particular location, since the Soviets use it to test the reliability of their satellite-decommissioning systems. In other words, watch the skies--it doesn't take our world-leading surveillance systems to know you don't want the Soviets to dump all their space-trash on top of you.
=坠落在这些岛屿上的航天器，比所有科幻大片里出现的航天器加起来还要多。所以我们一定要充分利用这份资源。在此地与苏联抵抗运动那位人见人爱的甜心维拉交战时，我们强烈建议您保持机动——因为苏联正在此地测试他们的卫星报废系统是否可靠。换句话说，多留意天上——不用麻烦我们世界顶尖的侦察系统，您也知道自己肯定不想让苏联把太空垃圾全砸在头上吧。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_08]
Try to imagine, if you will, a battlefield--nay, a world--in which you had ample resources with which to build many, many tanks in order to defeat rival commanders attempting to do the same, in a grand tournament set on an island arena. Our greatest tacticians often say that the tank battle is the essence of modern warfare, and so this territory was created in order to test that theory in a very literal way. We are curious to see how you will fare.
不妨试想这样一片战场——不，是一方天地：您手握充足资源，能够造出不计其数的坦克，以此击败其他同样厉兵秣马的敌方指挥官。这场宏大竞技，便在一座岛屿竞技场上展开。我们最顶尖的战术家常说，坦克战是现代战争的精髓，而这片领地正是为以最直观的方式验证这一理论而设。我们很想知道，您能交出怎样的答卷。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_09]
Commander Lydia is one of the Allies' top brains, and here she is gathering soil samples for some of her latest science experiments. You see, this territory is unusually low on ore... The implications of an ore-free world are deeply disturbing so we had best get to the bottom of this before she does. In order to avoid any setbacks, we are granting you 10 times the usual amount of credits for this operation. Best spend them wisely. As for Lydia, she is said to be less conservative than she looks. But even she cannot spend forever.
莉迪雅指挥官是盟军最顶尖的智囊之一，她正在此地收集土壤样本，用于她最新的科学实验。您也看到了，这片区域的矿石储量异常稀少……一个没有矿石的世界，光是想想就令人深感不安，所以我们最好赶在她之前查个水落石出。为了确保行动万无一失，我们会给您拨付平常10倍的资金。请务必精打细算。至于莉迪雅，据说她并不像外表看上去那么保守——不过，就算是她，也不可能永远挥霍下去。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_10]
As the Soviets, Allies, and Empire of the Rising Sun continued to wage their petty wars, quite a rivalry developed between the leading women on each side. Now each of these spirited young ladies is convinced that she is superior, and so the three of them are having it out in the middle of nowhere, with little but their pride at stake. Why not go check up on them to make sure everybody's playing fair? We're as curious as they are to see who the real winner is. Just make sure she goes home a loser like the rest of them.
苏联、盟军和旭日帝国之间的战事仍在继续，而各方阵营中的领军女性之间，也悄然形成了激烈的竞争关系。如今，这三位意气风发的年轻女性都坚信自己才是最强者，于是她们决定在荒郊野外一决高下，赌上各自的尊严。您何不去瞧瞧，确保没有人耍花招？我们和她们一样好奇，究竟谁才是真正的赢家。不过请务必让那个赢家最后也和其他人一样，灰溜溜地回家。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_11]
This is where many of the Allies' spies-in-training practice the arts of disguise, infiltration, bribing, swimming, smooth-talking, bowtie-tying, and more. Luckily for you, weapons training isn't part of the regimen, so if you play your cards right, it should be no trouble for you to wipe all those smug looks off of their faces. Be sure to do the same to Commander Giles, their supervising officer. We should be able to gain considerable insight into the Allies' spy network as a result of this particular operation.
这里就是许多盟军受训间谍练习化妆、渗透、贿赂、游泳、花言巧语、打领带等技艺的地方。幸运的是，武器训练并非他们的课程内容，所以只要您应对得当，扫平他们脸上那副自鸣得意的表情应该不在话下。别忘了让他们的主管军官吉尔利斯指挥官也尝尝同样的滋味。此次行动若能成功，我们将得以深入了解盟军的间谍网络。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_12]
This island region is used to train Imperial Shinobi in infiltration, assassination, meditation, intimidation, evasion, succinct speech, and long-distance swimming. Unfortunately for the Empire, the Soviets happen to be testing a new waterproofing compound for its Terror Drones here as well. That means we have an invaluable opportunity here to survey both Imperial and Soviet close-quarters combat tactics. We strongly advise building plenty of fortress walls and anti-infantry defenses to keep your opponents at bay.
这座岛屿用于训练帝国忍者，修习渗透、刺杀、静心调息、威慑、遁逃、言辞简练与长距离泅渡之术。不过，帝国的运气不怎么好，因为苏联也在这里测试恐怖机器人的新防水涂层。对我们来说，这是个大好机会，我们可以同时调查帝国和苏联的近战战术。我们强烈建议您建造大量的围墙和反步兵防御，用来困住您的对手。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_13]
Although the Imperial Commander Shinzo is seen as a stern traditionalist, his dedication to the Way of the Warrior is greater still. So he is here at his island retreat, learning the virtues of one of the Empire's most advanced vehicles, the Sea-Wing. It would be awfully nice to know exactly how its propulsion systems work, and how its pilots manage to avoid motion sickness. So why don't you go take a look for us?

So of course Commander Moskvin was first on the scene so that he could try out his new Desolator Trooper divisions and make sure they truly are immune to environmental catastrophes. Although the Desolator Troopers are by all means vile, their ability to withstand extremely toxic conditions is intriguing, so it would be good of you to investigate.
这里发生了一场严重的工业事故，牵扯到好几座超级反应堆、一批爆炸桶、一名征召兵，还有一个恐怖机器人。不用多说，莫斯克文指挥官自然是第一个赶到现场的正好拿他的新化学部队来试试，看看他们是不是真的能在这种环境灾难中毫发无伤。化学部队虽然确实令人作呕，但他们抵御极端毒性环境的能力却相当引人好奇，您不妨去查探一番。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_39]
This is the complex in which the ever-resourceful Soviet military came up with the idea for the Grinder, after a construction accident involving a souped-up steamroller resulted in eight deaths, 37 injuries, and several million rubles' worth of property damage.  The ever-delicate Commander Oleg is here putting the Grinders through the paces. Historical data shows that he isn't going to give them up without a fight.
当初一场建筑事故造成八死三十七伤，外加几百万卢布的财产损失，肇事的是一辆改装过的蒸汽压路机。事故过后，足智多谋的苏联军方就是在这片厂区捣鼓出了粉碎者的构想。如今一向讲究的欧列格指挥官正在此地让粉碎者进行各项测试。从以往记录来看，他是不会乖乖交出来的，少不了一场硬仗。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_40]
A scheduled service update in our central cryosystems has caused one of the Allies' orbital cryo satellites to become unstable. Their commanding officer in this region is none too pleased, for we have arranged for her to be held accountable for this problem. Expect to face the full brunt of our cryo-capable weaponry here. We must ensure this never happens again.
我们中央冰冻系统的一次例行服务更新，导致盟军其中一颗冰冻卫星变得很不稳定。驻守该地区的指挥官对此极为不满，因为我们已经安排好让她来为此事承担责任。您在此地将要面对各种冷冻武器的全面攻击。我们绝不能让类似的事再次发生。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_41]
After you gave Commander Hill his first thrashing in more than a decade, he started practicing again, getting back to what he considers "tip-top shape." He wants a rematch, and who are we to deny such a thing to such a kindly old man? More importantly, the Powers That Be want to see how you fare against him and one of our latest designs. They suspect it might be too advanced for someone like him. Perhaps you think you can handle it, though...?
自从您狠狠修理了希尔指挥官后，希尔重新开始训练，希望能够恢复最佳状态。他渴望再次与您对决，我们怎能拒绝这位指挥官的挑战呢？更重要的是，高层希望观察您如何应对他，以及我们最新装备的表现。对这位老先生来说，这套新装备可能过于先进。不过，对您这样的指挥官而言，应该不成问题吧……？

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_42]
Commander Giles seemed a little distressed after you abruptly ended his partnership with Commander Vera, so now he is off sulking in Central America, trying to act important from behind a desk. We didn't mean to push him out of the fight altogether, so why don't you provoke him into another little scuffle? Besides, it will give you an opportunity to square off against one of the new mercenary squadrons working for the Allies in the area. Defeat them and one of our finest new aircraft will be yours.
自从您突然终结了吉尔利斯指挥官和维拉指挥官的合作关系，他似乎一直郁郁寡欢。如今他跑到中美洲去生闷气了，整天窝在办公桌后面装出一副日理万机的样子。我们无意把他彻底赶出战局，您何不去激他一下，让他再跟您打一场小规模的交手？这也正好给您一个机会，去看看该地区为盟军效力的其中一支佣兵中队。击败他们，我们最新的一款飞机就是您的了。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_43]
This place is often used for testing of some of the world's deadliest and costliest weapons. Due to all the demand, such weapons come fast and cheap here, so you had best make good use of them quickly. Commander Oleg is already on the scene, trying to wrap his thick head around some of the Soviets' own special weapons. Why not run him out of town? It's best not to have to share an area such as this.
这个地方经常被用来测试一些世界上最致命、最昂贵的武器。由于需求量巨大，这些武器在这里生产得又快又便宜，您最好抓紧时间好好利用它们。欧列格指挥官已经到场了，正试图用他那颗笨脑袋去弄明白苏联的一些特种武器。何不干脆把他赶走？这种地方最好还是别跟人分享。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_44]
This was the site of a particularly fierce battle in which the Soviet Union's infamous Natasha wound up shooting the pilots out of dozens of different vehicles. And now, Commander Vera is trying to clean up the mess, while her rival Takara threatens to snatch up as many operational vehicles as possible. Do not wait for them to settle in. Get your forces to those vehicles as quickly as you can, and drive Vera and Takara out. There's a treasure trove of forensic evidence waiting for us here.
这里曾爆发过一场异常激烈的战斗，苏联那位恶名昭著的娜塔莎在那场战斗中射杀了数十辆各式载具的驾驶员。如今维拉指挥官正在收拾残局，而她的死对头高良则虎视眈眈，想把尽可能多还能运作的载具偷走。别等她们站稳脚跟。尽快派部队去占领那些载具，把维拉和高良赶出去。这里有一大批现场证据等着我们去发掘。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_45]
The temperamental young Moskvin has taken over a strange old castle in the middle of nowhere and is using it to conduct all kinds of twisted military experiments. As the son of a late Soviet experimental technology minister, Moskvin's knowledge and tactics are valuable to us, so we need you to go ahead and engage him. Unfortunately this place is too close to the Soviet heartland so we're going to fly in under the radar. Or not fly at all, to be exact. You'll have to make do without air support.
喜怒无常的年轻指挥官莫斯克文占领了某处一座古怪的老旧城堡，正在里面进行各种扭曲的军事实验。作为苏联已故实验技术部长的儿子，莫斯克文的知识和战术对我们来说相当有价值，所以需要您去对付他。可惜这片区域离苏联腹地太近了，我们只能保持隐身潜入。准确地说，根本飞不进去。您只能在没有空中支援的情况下完成任务了。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_46]
In her spare time, your bespectacled friend Commander Lydia enjoys attempting to improve upon the homogenous residual-interactive neutron kinetic (or "S.H.R.I.N.K.") technology found in the Allied Cryocopter. Unfortunately, this goes against the end-user licensing agreement dictating that the technology must not be reverse-engineered under any circumstances, and her experiments are already creating some irritating problems. We would appreciate it if you taught her a lesson in reading the fine print.
闲暇时候，您那位戴眼镜的好朋友莉迪雅指挥官，总喜欢琢磨如何改进盟军冷冻直升机上的同质残余交互中子动能技术（缩小光束技术）。可惜的是，这违反了最终用户许可协议，协议明确规定在任何情况下都不得对该技术进行逆向工程。她的实验已经给我们造成了一些麻烦。希望您能去给她上一课，教她好好读读条款细则。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_47]
Commanders Shinzo and Hill have fought on nearly a dozen occasions in the past, and even now they're prepare to have at it again, out in the middle of the Pacific. So get ready to get wet; we rarely have the opportunity to examine the Imperial and Allied navies in such a pristine, controlled environment. Because those two are carrying an old grudge, they'll surely direct most of their pent-up aggression towards each other. Just be ready to clean up after them.
晋三和希尔这两位指挥官过去已经交手过不下十次，如今他们又准备在太平洋中央再打一场。您得做好下海的准备了。我们很少有机会在如此纯粹、受控的环境中同时观察帝国和盟军的海军实力。那两人积怨已久，肯定会把憋了多年的火气都撒在对方身上。您只需等他们打完，再出手收拾残局。

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_48]
The Allies are working on some key advancements to the Chronosphere tech they've come to rely on, and you're going to have a look at what they're up to. Make sure their research suffers some unfortunate setbacks while you're at it, though be careful--Commander Lydia is here, and after the last time you dealt with her, supposedly she's been real keen to have another go against you. Don't expect her to go as quietly this time, and mind that Chronosphere of hers...
盟军正在对他们赖以仰仗的超时空传送仪技术进行一些关键性的升级改良，您得去探探他们的进展。顺便给他们的研究制造一点“意外”挫折。不过要当心——莉迪雅指挥官就在那里。据说自从上次跟您交过手之后，她一直特别想再跟您打一场。这次别指望她会轻易收场，还得提防她手上的超时空传送仪……

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_49]
The Soviets' latest, careless attempt to meddle with new military technology is code-named URSA MAJOR, and we need you to put an end to it here before the program reaches fruition. This program is the pet project of that oaf Commander Oleg, who will be a nuisance if you do not take care of him quickly, along with whatever this URSA MAJOR thing is. Best to proceed with caution...
苏联最近又一次鲁莽地试图染指新型军事技术，这次行动的代号叫“巨熊”。我们需要您赶在这项计划大功告成之前把它彻底终结。这个计划是欧列格指挥官那个呆子的心头宝贝，如果不尽快把他和那个什么“巨熊”一起收拾掉，他会成为一个大麻烦。务必谨慎行事……

[SCENARIO:DESCRIPTION_50]
Sorry to break this to you, but we don't expect you to succeed here. Please don't take it personally. Nevertheless, you are cordially invited to square off against all three of the Allies' commanders, who have worked out a strategy that they say you will never be able to defeat; and we believe the threat is credible. For what it's worth, in the unlikely event of your victory, our finest prototype will be yours.
恕我直言，我们其实没指望您能打赢这一仗。还请勿介怀。不过，我们还是诚挚地邀请您去跟盟军的三位指挥官较量一番——他们自称已经制定出一套您绝对破解不了的战术，而且我们觉得他们的威胁是可信的。话虽如此，万一奇迹发生，您最终取胜，我们最精良的原型机就是您的了。

To construct a Power Plant, open your Production Structures Tab and click on the Power Plant button. Then when it's ready, click on the button again, then click on open terrain to place the Power Plant.
要建造发电厂，打开生产建筑标签并点击发电厂按钮。准备就绪后再次点击该按钮，然后点击空旷地形来放置发电厂。

[SCRIPT:T02_CONSTRUCT_REFINERY]
To build an Ore Refinery, open your Production Structures Tab and click on the Ore Refinery button. Once it's ready, click on its button again and click on the open terrain just in front of the Ore Mine to place it in an optimal position.
要建造矿石精炼厂，打开生产建筑标签并点击矿石精炼厂按钮。准备就绪后再次点击该按钮，然后点击矿脉正前方的空旷地形，将其放置在最理想的位置。

要启动侦察扫描，左键点击第一个位置设定扫描起点，然后左键点击第二个位置设定扫描轨迹。

To use the Reverse-Move command, select units that can move in reverse and press the "D" key.

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT3]

要命令单位倒退移动，先选中单位，按“D”键，然后右键点击目标位置。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT4]
To instantly move the camera to the most recent radar event, press the space bar.
要快速将镜头移至最近雷达事件位置，按空格键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT5]
To instantly move the camera back to base, press the "H" key.
要快速将镜头移回基地，按“H”键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT6]
To assign units to a Control Group, select the units, hold the control key, and press a number key.
要将单位编入编组，先选中单位，按住Ctrl键，然后按数字键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT7]
To access an assigned Control Group, press the corresponding number key.
要选中已设置的编组，按对应的数字键即可。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT8]
To instantly snap the camera to a control group, double-tap the corresponding number key.
要快速将镜头跳转至编组，快速连按两次对应的数字键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT9]
To issue a Crush Move command, press the "G" key and right-click on a destination.
要下达碾压移动指令，按"G"键，然后右键点击目标位置。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT10]
To open the Advanced Commands panel, click on the button near the unit portrait in the bottom-right of the screen.
要打开高级指令面板，点击屏幕右下角单位头像旁边的按钮。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT11]
To set a unit to Hold Ground stance, click on the Hold Ground button in the Advanced Commands panel.
要将单位设为固守姿态，在高级指令面板中点击固守姿态按钮。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT12]
To adequately defend the base, set three Guardian Tanks to Hold Ground stance and move them to the locations designated on the radar.
要有效防御基地，将三辆守护者坦克设为固守状态，并移动到雷达上标记的位置。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT13]
To build a Multigunner Turret, select the Support Structures button on the sidebar and click on the appropriate structure icon.
要建造多功能炮塔，点击侧边栏的支援建筑按钮，然后点击对应的建筑图标。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT14]
To construct a wall, build and place a Fortress Wall piece from the Support Structures queue, then build and place a second wall when green build grid indicators show a connection between the two wall segments.
要建造围墙，先从支援建筑队列中建造并放置一段围墙，然后当绿色建造网格指示两段墙体可以连接时，再建造并放置第二段。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT15]
To select all units on the battlefield, press the "Q" key.
要选择战场上所有单位，按"Q"键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT16]
To cycle between unit types, press the Tab key when multiple unit types are selected.
要在已选中的多种单位类型间循环切换，选中多种单位后按Tab键。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT17]
To use Waypoint Mode, select units and hold the Alt key while issuing move and attack orders.
要使用路径点模式，选中单位后按住Alt键，同时下达移动和攻击指令。

[SCRIPT:T05_TEXT18]
To see suppression in action, use the Javelin Soldiers to attack the garrisoned structure.
要查看压制效果的实际作用，使用标枪兵攻击驻守中的建筑。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_ATTACKMOVE]
To issue an Attack-Move command, select your units and press the A key. Then right-click on open terrain near your enemy's units.
要下达行进攻击指令，选中您的单位并按A键，然后右键点击敌军单位附近的空旷地形。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_ATTACKUNITS]

要攻击敌方单位，选中您的单位，然后右键点击敌人。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_MOVECAMERA]
To move your camera, hold down the right mouse button and move your mouse around.
要移动视角，按住鼠标右键并拖动鼠标。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_MOVEUNITS]
To move your units, select them and right-click on open terrain.
要移动您的单位，选中它们，然后右键点击空旷地形。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_ROTATECAMERA]
To rotate your camera, hold down the middle mouse button and move your mouse around.
要旋转视角，按住鼠标中键并拖动鼠标。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_SELECTUNITS]
To select your units, left-click on them. You may also hold down the left mouse button on open terrain and drag your mouse to create a selection box around your units.
要选择您的单位，左键点击它们。您也可以在空旷地形上按住左键并拖动鼠标，框选您的单位。

[SCRIPT:TUTORIAL_ZOOMCAMERA]
To zoom your camera, move your mouse wheel back and forth.
要缩放视角，前后滚动鼠标滚轮。

[SLASH_ERROR:NOTFOUND]
Slash command not found. Type '/help' for a list of commands and additional information.
找不到斜线命令。输入“/help”可查看命令列表及更多信息。

[SLASH_ERROR:PLAYERNOTFOUND]
Cannot send message. That player is currently unavailable.
无法发送消息。该玩家当前不在线。

[SLASH_HELP:ACCEPT]
accept <player> - Accept a friend request from a player.
accept <玩家名> - 接受一位玩家的好友请求。

[SLASH_HELP:AFK]
afk - Sets your status to Away From Keyboard. Players who send you messages while in this state will be told you are Away From Keyboard.
afk - 将您的状态设为“暂离”。其他玩家向您发送消息时会收到您“暂离”的提示。

[SLASH_HELP:FRIEND]
friend <player> - Invite a player to join your friends list.
friend <玩家名> - 邀请一位玩家加入您的好友列表。

[SLASH_HELP:GENERALHELP]
Available commands are: help, tell, reply, friend, ignore, me, players, afk
可用命令有：help, tell, reply, friend, ignore, me, players, afk

[SLASH_HELP:HELP]
help <command> - Displays more information about the selected command.
help <命令名> - 显示有关所选命令的更多信息。

[SLASH_HELP:IGNORE]
ignore <player> - Toggle ignore status of another player. You will not see messages from ignored players.
ignore <玩家名> - 切换对某玩家的屏蔽状态。被屏蔽玩家发送的消息将不会显示。

[SLASH_HELP:INVITE]
invite <player> - Invite a player to join the match lobby you are in.
invite <玩家名> - 邀请一位玩家加入您所在的比赛房间。

[SLASH_HELP:ME]
me  - Use to send an emote. For example, try '/me thirsts for battle!'
me - 发送表情动作。例如：/me thirsts for battle!！

[SLASH_HELP:PLAYERS]
players - Display number of players online.
players - 显示在线玩家数量。

[SLASH_HELP:QUIT]
quit - Quit back to Main menu.
quit - 返回主菜单。

[SLASH_HELP:REJECT]
reject <player> - Decline an invitation to join a match lobby.
reject <玩家名> - 拒绝加入比赛房间的邀请。

[SLASH_HELP:REPLY]
reply - Reply to last private message received.
reply - 回复最近收到的私聊消息。

[SLASH_HELP:STATS]
stats <player> - View selected player's stats.
stats <玩家名> - 查看指定玩家的战绩。

[SLASH_HELP:TELL]
tell <player> - Send private message to selected player.
tell <玩家名> - 向指定玩家发送私聊消息。

[SLASH_MSG:AFKEND]
You are no longer Away From Keyboard.
您已解除“暂离”状态。

[SLASH_MSG:AFKRESPONSE]
%hs is currently Away From Keyboard. Please try again later.
%hs 当前处于“暂离”状态。请稍后再试。

[SLASH_MSG:AFKSTART]
You are now Away From Keyboard. Type any message to return.
您现在处于“暂离”状态。输入任意消息解除。

[SLASH_MSG:CANNOTFINDPLAYER]
Command failed, unable to find player '%hs'.
命令失败，找不到玩家 %hs。

[SLASH_MSG:INVITENOTINGAME]
You can only invite players from within a match lobby. Please create or join a match.
您只能在比赛房间内邀请玩家。请创建或加入一场比赛。

[SLASH_MSG:PLAYERS]
There are %d players online and %d matches in progress.
当前有 %d 位玩家在线，%d 场比赛正在进行中。

[SUBTITLE:AFS01_01]
[2.5:1.5]Welcome back, Commander.[4.4:1.8]You're looking very fit and rested.[6.7:3.2]I hope you're ready, because we have some important work ahead of us.[10.5:3.9]Our occupation force in Japan has intercepted some disturbing news.[14.9:2.7]Instability is mounting  in various provinces there,[17.9:2.8]threatening everything that we achieved in our victory.[21.2:3.9]Their leader, Tatsu, has been cooperative with our postwar arrangements,[25.4:2.5]but the problem lies with their provincial Shoguns.
[2.5:1.5]欢迎归来，指挥官。[4.4:1.8]您看起来气色不错，精力充沛。[6.7:3.2]希望您已做好准备，因为有几项重要的任务等着我们。[10.5:3.9]我们在日本的占领军截获了一些令人不安的消息。[14.9:5.8]日本各地局势日趋不稳，对我们来之不易的胜利成果构成了威胁。[21.2:6.7]他们的领袖达郎一直配合我们的战后安排，但问题出在那些地方将军身上。

[SUBTITLE:AFS01_02]
[28.5:4.2]These warlords are raising armies against Tatsu, and our occupation.[33.2:2.8]The plot seems to be spearheaded by Shinzo Nagama.[36.5:4.6]His loyalties lie fiercely with the late Emperor, and he refuses to bow to any other.[41.5:3.4]We'll need to send in our own forces to put this uprising to rest.[45.3:2.5]You'll be working directly with Tatsu on this.[48.3:1.9]Please try to show him the proper respect, sir,[50.2:2.4]as he's working with us now.[52.6:1.4]Good luck, commander.
[28.5:4.2]这些军阀正在集结军队，反抗达郎和我们的占领军。[33.2:2.8]这场叛乱的带头者似乎是长间晋三。[36.5:4.6]他誓死效忠已故的天皇，拒绝向任何人低头。[41.5:3.4]我们必须派兵镇压这场叛乱。[45.3:2.5]您将在此行动中与达郎直接合作。[48.3:4.3]请给予他应有的尊重，长官，因为他现在是我们的盟友。[52.6:1.4]祝您好运，指挥官。

[SUBTITLE:AFS01_03]
[57.6:4.7]Commander, I'm told that you're the best field officer the Allies have.[62.6:3.1]I hope so, because we need to move quickly.[66.1:4.3]Shinzo has many loyal followers and his numbers are growing steadily.[70.8:3.5]He has always resented my authority and my progressive ways.[74.8:4.1]He's too old and stubborn to understand that the world has changed.[79.4:2.5]But that doesn't mean he isn't dangerous.[82.6:4.2]He is a master warrior and quite deadly.[87.6:3.3]You'll find him in the Iga Province, home of the Nagama clan.[91.5:4.9]Do not let his stubbornness be the cause of our destruction.
[57.6:4.7]指挥官，我听说您是盟军中最出色的战地指挥官。[62.6:3.1]希望如此，因为我们必须尽快行动。[66.1:4.3]晋三有很多忠实的追随者，他的势力正在不断壮大。[70.8:3.5]他一直对我的权威和改革方式心怀不满。[74.8:4.1]他太老了，也太顽固了，无法认清世界已经改变。[79.4:8.1]但这并不意味着他不危险。他是一名强大的武士，而且极其致命。[87.6:3.3]您会在伊贺国找到他，那里是长间家族的故土。[91.5:4.9]不要让他那顽固的性格害了我们。

[SUBTITLE:AFS02_01]
[4.4:2.4]You haven't lost your touch, Commander.[7.3:4.9]Shinzo's armies have sworn allegiance to Tatsu, so now that area is secured.[12.5:4.2]However, there are still other Shogunate leaders who are yet unwilling to back down.[17.4:5.3]Despite Shinzo's defeat, The Warlord Kenji in Osaka continues to marshal his forces.[23.1:2.6]Tatsu will give you the particulars on your assignment.[26.3:2]Do be careful, sir.
[4.4:2.4]您仍旧宝刀未老，指挥官。[7.3:4.9]晋三的军队已经向达郎宣誓效忠，那片区域已经平定。[12.5:4.2]不过，仍有其他幕府将领不愿就此罢手。[17.4:5.3]尽管晋三已经败了，大阪的军阀贤治仍在集结兵力。[23.1:2.6]达郎会把任务细节交代给您。[26.3:2]千万小心，长官。

[SUBTITLE:AFS02_02]
[31.6:5.2]Kenji has always been arrogant, but I never thought he would go this far and betray me.[37.6:2.5]He might prove to be even a greater challenge than Shinzo,[40.6:3.4]as he is a man who has embraced the modern ways of warfare.[44.5:2.8]He will use the latest technology to protect his position.[47.8:3.3]It's a heavily defended high-tech fortress outside of Osaka.[51.9:2.3]Do not under-estimate him.[54.3:5.5]Kenji may seem brash and arrogant, but make no mistake, he is lethal.
[31.6:5.2]贤治向来傲慢，但我从没想过他会走到这一步，背叛我。[37.6:6.4]他可能比晋三更难对付，因为他接受的是现代战争理念。[44.5:2.8]他会用最先进的技术来守卫自己的据点。[47.8:3.3]那是大阪城外一座重兵防守的高科技要塞。[51.9:2.3]别轻视他，他非常危险。[54.3:5.5]贤治或许行事鲁莽，但他绝对是一个致命的对手。

[SUBTITLE:AFS03_01]
[4.1:1.7]Good job, Commander.[6.3:4.4]Thanks to your brilliance on the battlefield, the rebellion is very close to being contained.[11.1:3]There is only one Shogun left who is left to surrender.[14.3:3.2]Takara is planning a full scale assault upon the throne.[17.7:1.6]She needs to be stopped, sir.[19.6:3.6]I know your men are tired and your equipment has been pushed to the limit.[23.4:3.6]But this is our last chance to prevent this region from falling into chaos.[27.3:2.3]Tatsu will brief you on the specifics.[30:1]Good luck.
[4.1:1.7]干得好，指挥官。[6.3:4.4]多亏您在战场上的出色表现，叛军已接近平定。[11.1:3]如今只剩一位将军仍未投降。[14.3:3.2]高良计划发动全面进攻，夺取王权。[17.7:1.6]必须设法阻止她，长官。[19.6:3.6]我知道您的部队已疲惫不堪，装备也损耗严重。[23.4:3.6]但这是我们阻止该地区陷入混乱的最后机会。[27.3:2.3]达郎会向您说明具体任务。[30:1]祝您好运。

By now you may have heard the stories of Yuriko Omega,[14.2:3.9]the mysterious Japanese girl with the terrifying psionic powers.[18.1:3.2]During the war, Allied soldiers claimed that she decimated[21.2:3.6]entire combat battalions using only her mind.[24.8:3]Who is this young girl? Where did she come from?[27.8:4]And how did she develop these fearsome and fantastic abilities?[31.8:2.4]Yuriko's history is somewhat murky.[34.2:3.3]Some sources say that her real name is Yuriko Matsui,[37.6:3.6]and that Omega is simply a code name used by the military.[41.2:4.9]According to these same sources she was born in the small coastal town of Tanabe[46.1:3.9]and exhibited paranormal powers from the time she was very young.
[9.4:8.7]谢谢你，泰德。各位或许已经听说过欧米伽百合子的故事，那个拥有可怕心灵能力的日本少女。[18.1:6.6]战争期间，盟军士兵都说她仅凭意念便能摧毁整支作战部队。[24.8:7]这个少女是谁？她来自何方？她又是如何获得这些可怕而惊人的能力？[31.8:2.4]百合子的身世相当模糊。[34.2:6.9]有消息称她的本名是松井百合子，“欧米伽”只是军方使用的代号。[41.2:8.8]同一消息来源称她出生于沿海小镇田边，自幼便展现出超自然能力。

[1.3:4.2]欢迎回到我们关于欧米伽百合子的特别报道。[5.7:4]战争期间，百合子被帝国军方当作武器使用。[9.7:5.3]她的心灵能力使她成为帝国军械库中最危险的武器之一。[15.3:3.4]盟军攻入东京后，她那令人闻风丧胆的日子才终于结束。[19.2:5.3]百合子在皇宫废墟下被发现时已失去意识，随后被收押。[24.8:7.7]被运往西方后，她被完全隔离，关押在关岛丛林深处一座戒备森严的设施中……

Force the game to redetect your NAT/Firewall configuration.  Choose this option after changing a Firewall or NAT
强制重新检测您的NAT/防火墙配置。在更改防火墙或NAT后选择此选项。

Toggle showing 3D shadows in game. Turn off for improved performance

[TOOLTIP:OPTIONS/TOOLTIPANISOTROPICTEXTUREFILTERING]
Enable sharper textures if your video card supports this feature.  Turn off for improved performance

[TOOLTIP:OPTIONS/TOOLTIPBRIGHTNESS]

[TOOLTIP:OPTIONS/TOOLTIPDETAIL]
Adjusting details too high may slow  the gameplay for lower end PCs.  This setting can only be changed  from the Options in the Main Menu

[TOOLTIP:OPTIONS/TOOLTIPDISPLAYFOREIGNLANGUAGE]

[TOOLTIP:OPTIONS/TOOLTIPDOUBLECLICKGUARD]
Adjust to allow attack move, then guard area  by double right clicking destination on battlefield
调整设置，允许通过双击右键执行攻击移动并警戒区域。在战场上右键单击目标位置。
